    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- type probes
    stdout| def _is_mapping(value):
    stdout|     if value is True or value is False or value is None:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         value.keys
    stdout|     except AttributeError:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     return True
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _is_array(value):
    stdout|     if value is True or value is False or value is None:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     if _is_mapping(value):
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         value.append
    stdout|     except AttributeError:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     return True
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _is_str(value):
    stdout|     if value is True or value is False or value is None:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     if _is_mapping(value) or _is_array(value):
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         value.lower
    stdout|     except AttributeError:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     return True
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _is_json_bool(value):
    stdout|     return value is True or value is False
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _is_number(value):
    stdout|     if _is_json_bool(value) or value is None:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     if _is_mapping(value) or _is_array(value) or _is_str(value):
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     return True
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- numerics
    stdout| def _move_matrix(pairs, n):
    stdout|     rows = []
    stdout|     for (i, j) in pairs:
    stdout|         row = [0] * n
    stdout|         row[i] += 1
    stdout|         row[j] += 1
    stdout|         rows.append(row)
    stdout|     return rows
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _matrix_rank(rows, n):
    stdout|     mat = [[Fraction(x) for x in row] for row in rows]
    stdout|     nrows = len(mat)
    stdout|     rank = 0
    stdout|     for col in range(n):
    stdout|         pivot = None
    stdout|         for r in range(rank, nrows):
    stdout|             if mat[r][col] != 0:
    stdout|                 pivot = r
    stdout|                 break
    stdout|         if pivot is None:
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         mat[rank], mat[pivot] = mat[pivot], mat[rank]
    stdout|         f = mat[rank][col]
    stdout|         mat[rank] = [x / f for x in mat[rank]]
    stdout|         for r in range(nrows):
    stdout|             if r != rank and mat[r][col] != 0:
    stdout|                 factor = mat[r][col]
    stdout|                 mat[r] = [mat[r][k] - factor * mat[rank][k] for k in range(n)]
    stdout|         rank += 1
    stdout|     return rank
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _kernel_dim(pairs, n):
    stdout|     if n <= 0:
    stdout|         return 0
    stdout|     return n - _matrix_rank(_move_matrix(pairs, n), n)
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _kernel_vector(pairs, n):
    stdout|     """Return a nonzero kernel vector (alternating ±1) if it is a genuine kernel vector, else None."""
    stdout|     if n <= 0:
    stdout|         return None
    stdout|     cand = [1 if k % 2 == 0 else -1 for k in range(n)]
    stdout|     for row in _move_matrix(pairs, n):
    stdout|         s = 0
    stdout|         for k in range(n):
    stdout|             s += row[k] * cand[k]
    stdout|         if s != 0:
    stdout|             return None
    stdout|     return cand
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _pinned_pairs():
    stdout|     return set(tuple(sorted(p)) for p in E3_MOVE_PAIRS)
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _supplied_pairs(mpairs):
    stdout|     out = set()
    stdout|     for p in mpairs:
    stdout|         if _is_array(p) and len(p) == 2:
    stdout|             i = p[0]
    stdout|             j = p[1]
    stdout|             if _is_number(i) and _is_number(j):
    stdout|                 out.add(tuple(sorted((int(i), int(j)))))
    stdout|     return out
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _vector_in_kernel(w, pairs, n):
    stdout|     for row in _move_matrix(pairs, n):
    stdout|         if _dot(row, w) != 0:
    stdout|             return False
    stdout|     return True
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _dot(vec_a, vec_b):
    stdout|     s = 0
    stdout|     for k in range(len(vec_a)):
    stdout|         s += vec_a[k] * vec_b[k]
    stdout|     return s
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _same_line(a, b):
    stdout|     """True iff a and b are nonzero and are scalar multiples (same kernel line)."""
    stdout|     if not a or not b:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     if len(a) != len(b):
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     flag_a = None
    stdout|     for v in a:
    stdout|         if v != 0:
    stdout|             flag_a = v
    stdout|             break
    stdout|     if flag_a is None:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     ratio = None
    stdout|     for v in b:
    stdout|         if v != 0:
    stdout|             ratio = v / flag_a
    stdout|             break
    stdout|     if ratio is None:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     for k in range(len(a)):
    stdout|         if a[k] == 0 and b[k] != 0:
    stdout|             return False
    stdout|         if a[k] != 0 and b[k] != a[k] * ratio:
    stdout|             return False
    stdout|     return True
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- bundle schema
    stdout| def _validate_bundle(bundle):
    stdout|     """Raise ValueError on malformed bundle structure (before any traversal)."""
    stdout|     if not _is_mapping(bundle):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("bundle must be a JSON object")
    stdout|     if bundle.get("schema") != BUNDLE_SCHEMA:
    stdout|         raise ValueError("unsupported bundle schema: %r" % (bundle.get("schema"),))
    stdout|     if not _is_str(bundle.get("contract")):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("bundle must have a 'contract' string")
    stdout|     contract = bundle["contract"]
    stdout|     if contract not in PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS:
    stdout|         raise ValueError("unsupported contract: %r (expected E3/E4/E7)" % (contract,))
    stdout|     if not _is_mapping(bundle.get("candidate")):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("bundle must have a 'candidate' object")
    stdout|     if not _is_str(bundle["candidate"].get("id")):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("candidate must have a string 'id'")
    stdout|     claims = bundle.get("claims")
    stdout|     if claims is not None and not _is_array(claims):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("'claims' must be an array")
    stdout|     nodes = bundle.get("nodes")
    stdout|     if not _is_array(nodes):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("'nodes' must be an array")
    stdout| 
    stdout|     seen = {}
    stdout| 
    stdout|     def note_id(oid):
    stdout|         if oid in seen:
    stdout|             raise ValueError("duplicate id %r" % (oid,))
    stdout|         seen[oid] = True
    stdout| 
    stdout|     if claims is not None:
    stdout|         for c in claims:
    stdout|             if _is_mapping(c) and _is_str(c.get("id")):
    stdout|                 note_id(c["id"])
    stdout|     note_id(bundle["candidate"]["id"])
    stdout|     for nd in nodes:
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(nd):
    stdout|             raise ValueError("node must be an object")
    stdout|         nid = nd.get("id")
    stdout|         if not _is_str(nid):
    stdout|             raise ValueError("node must have a string 'id'")
    stdout|         note_id(nid)
    stdout|         cites = nd.get("cites")
    stdout|         if cites is None:
    stdout|             cites = nd.get("refs")
    stdout|         if cites is not None and not _is_array(cites):
    stdout|             raise ValueError("node %s cites must be an array" % (nid,))
    stdout|         if _is_array(cites):
    stdout|             for c in cites:
    stdout|                 if not _is_str(c):
    stdout|                     raise ValueError("node %s cite must be a string id" % (nid,))
    stdout|         if nd.get("payload") is not None and not _is_mapping(nd.get("payload")):
    stdout|             raise ValueError("node %s payload must be an object" % (nid,))
    stdout| 
    stdout|     records = bundle.get("records")
    stdout|     if records is not None and not _is_mapping(records):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("'records' must be an object")
    stdout|     if _is_mapping(records):
    stdout|         for rname, arr in records.items():
    stdout|             if not _is_array(arr):
    stdout|                 raise ValueError("records.%s must be an array" % (rname,))
    stdout|             for r in arr:
    stdout|                 if not _is_mapping(r):
    stdout|                     raise ValueError("records.%s entry must be an object" % (rname,))
    stdout|                 rid = r.get("id")
    stdout|                 if _is_str(rid):
    stdout|                     note_id(rid)
    stdout|                 cites = r.get("cites") or r.get("refs")
    stdout|                 if _is_array(cites):
    stdout|                     for c in cites:
    stdout|                         if not _is_str(c):
    stdout|                             raise ValueError("record cite must be a string id")
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- proof graph
    stdout| def _resolve_proof(bundle):
    stdout|     """Return (status, diagnostics). status[id] in {'assumption','ok','self','upstream','cycle'}."""
    stdout|     claims = bundle.get("claims") or []
    stdout|     nodes = bundle.get("nodes") or []
    stdout|     candidate = bundle.get("candidate")
    stdout|     records = bundle.get("records") or {}
    stdout|     items = {}
    stdout| 
    stdout|     def add(obj, kind):
    stdout|         if obj is None or not _is_mapping(obj):
    stdout|             return
    stdout|         oid = obj.get("id")
    stdout|         if not _is_str(oid):
    stdout|             return
    stdout|         items[oid] = (obj, kind)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     for c in claims:
    stdout|         add(c, "assumption")
    stdout|     add(candidate, "conclusion")
    stdout|     for nd in nodes:
    stdout|         kind = nd.get("kind")
    stdout|         add(nd, "assumption" if kind == "assumption" else "derived")
    stdout|     for rname, arr in records.items():
    stdout|         for r in arr:
    stdout|             add(r, "derived")
    stdout| 
    stdout|     all_ids = set(items)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     def cits(obj):
    stdout|         c = obj.get("cites")
    stdout|         if c is None:
    stdout|             c = obj.get("refs")
    stdout|         if c is None:
    stdout|             return []
    stdout|         return [x for x in c]
    stdout| 
    stdout|     memo = {}
    stdout| 
    stdout|     def resolve(oid, path):
    stdout|         if oid not in all_ids:
    stdout|             return "upstream"
    stdout|         if oid in memo and memo[oid] in ("ok", "assumption"):
    stdout|             return "ok"
    stdout|         if oid in memo and memo[oid] in ("self", "upstream", "cycle"):
    stdout|             return memo[oid]
    stdout|         obj, kind = items[oid]
    stdout|         if kind == "assumption":
    stdout|             memo[oid] = "assumption"
    stdout|             return "ok"
    stdout|         if oid in path:
    stdout|             memo[oid] = "cycle"
    stdout|             return "cycle"
    stdout|         cs = cits(obj)
    stdout|         if oid in cs:
    stdout|             memo[oid] = "self"
    stdout|             return "self"
    stdout|         if not cs:
    stdout|             memo[oid] = "upstream"
    stdout|             return "upstream"
    stdout|         for c in cs:
    stdout|             if c not in all_ids:
    stdout|                 memo[oid] = "upstream"
    stdout|                 return "upstream"
    stdout|         for c in cs:
    stdout|             r = resolve(c, path + [oid])
    stdout|             if r != "ok":
    stdout|                 memo[oid] = r
    stdout|                 return r
    stdout|         memo[oid] = "ok"
    stdout|         return "ok"
    stdout| 
    stdout|     for oid in list(items):
    stdout|         if oid not in memo:
    stdout|             resolve(oid, [])
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # Emit diagnostics once per id with a non-ok status.
    stdout|     diagnostics = []
    stdout|     emitted = set()
    stdout|     for oid in sorted(items):
    stdout|         st = memo.get(oid)
    stdout|         if st in ("self", "upstream", "cycle") and (oid, st) not in emitted:
    stdout|             emitted.add((oid, st))
    stdout|             if st == "self":
    stdout|                 diagnostics.append("self-citation in %s" % (oid,))
    stdout|             elif st == "cycle":
    stdout|                 diagnostics.append("circular support involving %s" % (oid,))
    stdout|             else:
    stdout|                 # distinguish unresolved-upstream reason
    stdout|                 cs = cits(items[oid][0])
    stdout|                 unknown = [c for c in cs if c not in all_ids]
    stdout|                 if unknown:
    stdout|                     diagnostics.append("unresolved upstream from %s: unknown id %s" % (oid, ",".join(unknown)))
    stdout|                 else:
    stdout|                     diagnostics.append("unresolved support %s (%s)" % (oid, st))
    stdout| 
    stdout|     return memo, diagnostics
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- support resolution
    stdout| def _support_by_role(bundle, status):
    stdout|     """Map role -> list of nodes whose status is 'ok' or 'assumption'."""
    stdout|     nodes = bundle.get("nodes") or []
    stdout|     records = bundle.get("records") or {}
    stdout|     known = {}
    stdout|     for nd in nodes:
    stdout|         if _is_mapping(nd) and _is_str(nd.get("id")):
    stdout|             known[nd["id"]] = nd
    stdout|     for rname, arr in records.items():
    stdout|         for r in arr:
    stdout|             if _is_mapping(r) and _is_str(r.get("id")):
    stdout|                 known[r["id"]] = r
    stdout|     by_role = {}
    stdout|     for cid in (bundle.get("candidate").get("cites") or bundle.get("candidate").get("refs") or []):
    stdout|         nd = known.get(cid)
    stdout|         if nd is None:
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         if status.get(cid) not in ("ok", "assumption"):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         role = nd.get("role")
    stdout|         if not role:
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         by_role.setdefault(role, []).append(nd)
    stdout|     return by_role
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- checked handler
    stdout| def _combine_set(evidence, citations, check, pass_key, fail_key, pass_causes, fail_causes):
    stdout|     """Set a check's evidence from accumulated pass/fail causes; both -> set both (grader exit 2)."""
    stdout|     if pass_causes and fail_causes:
    stdout|         evidence[check][pass_key] = True
    stdout|         evidence[check][fail_key] = True
    stdout|         citations.setdefault(pass_key, []).extend(pass_causes)
    stdout|         citations.setdefault(fail_key, []).extend(fail_causes)
    stdout|         citations.setdefault("contradictory:" + check, []).append("both pass and fail evidence derived")
    stdout|     elif pass_causes:
    stdout|         evidence[check][pass_key] = True
    stdout|         citations.setdefault(pass_key, []).extend(pass_causes)
    stdout|     elif fail_causes:
    stdout|         evidence[check][fail_key] = True
    stdout|         citations.setdefault(fail_key, []).extend(fail_causes)
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _weight_nodes(by_role):
    stdout|     return by_role.get("weights", [])
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _checked_e3(bundle, status):
    stdout|     """Derive E3 evidence by interpreting candidate payloads and computing, not authenticating roles."""
    stdout|     evidence = {c: {} for c in CHECKS}
    stdout|     citations = {}
    stdout|     diagnostics = []
    stdout|     by_role = _support_by_role(bundle, status)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # Determine the move family once (shared by C1/C2).
    stdout|     move_nodes = by_role.get("moves", [])
    stdout|     moves_supplied = None
    stdout|     moves_node_id = None
    stdout|     pinned = _pinned_pairs()
    stdout|     for nd in move_nodes:
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if _is_mapping(p) and "pairs" in p and _is_array(p["pairs"]):
    stdout|             moves_supplied = _supplied_pairs(p["pairs"])
    stdout|             moves_node_id = nd.get("id")
    stdout|             break
    stdout|     family_ok = moves_supplied is not None and moves_supplied == pinned
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # ---- C1 kernel & C2 preservation per weight record.
    stdout|     pass_c1, fail_c1, pass_c2, fail_c2 = [], [], [], []
    stdout|     for nd in _weight_nodes(by_role):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p) or "vector" not in p or not _is_array(p["vector"]):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         w = p["vector"]
    stdout|         if not all(_is_number(x) for x in w):
    stdout|             diagnostics.append("C1/C2: weight vector contains a non-number element")
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         if len(w) != E3_N:
    stdout|             diagnostics.append("C1/C2: weight length %s != pinned %s" % (len(w), E3_N))
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         if not family_ok:
    stdout|             continue  # cannot assess kernel/preservation against an incomplete/extra family
    stdout|         in_kernel = _vector_in_kernel(w, E3_MOVE_PAIRS, len(w))
    stdout|         ref = {"node": nd.get("id"), "field": "vector"}
    stdout|         if in_kernel:
    stdout|             pass_c1.append(ref)
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c1.append(ref)
    stdout|         # C2: per-move reading change.
    stdout|         all_zero = True
    stdout|         for q in E3_MOVE_PAIRS:
    stdout|             if w[q[0]] + w[q[1]] != 0:
    stdout|                 all_zero = False
    stdout|                 break
    stdout|         if all_zero:
    stdout|             pass_c2.append(ref)
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c2.append(ref)
    stdout|     if not family_ok:
    stdout|         diagnostics.append("supplied move family does not match pinned E3 problem (incomplete/extra)")
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C1", "derived_from_move_constraints",
    stdout|                  "stated_without_move_family", pass_c1, fail_c1)
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C2", "preserved_all_moves",
    stdout|                  "a_move_changes_reading", pass_c2, fail_c2)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # ---- C3 separation per weight+start.
    stdout|     pass_c3, fail_c3 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in _weight_nodes(by_role):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p) or "vector" not in p or not _is_array(p["vector"]):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         w = p["vector"]
    stdout|         if not all(_is_number(x) for x in w) or len(w) != E3_N:
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         for snd in by_role.get("start", []):
    stdout|             sp = snd.get("payload")
    stdout|             if not _is_mapping(sp) or "vector" not in sp or not _is_array(sp["vector"]):
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             sv = sp["vector"]
    stdout|             if len(sv) != E3_N or not all(_is_number(x) for x in sv):
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             sum_w = sum(w)
    stdout|             r_start = _dot(w, sv)
    stdout|             ref = {"node": nd.get("id"), "field": "vector"}
    stdout|             if sum_w == 0 and r_start != 0:
    stdout|                 pass_c3.append(ref)
    stdout|             else:
    stdout|                 fail_c3.append(ref)
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C3", "start_differs_from_target",
    stdout|                  "start_equals_target", pass_c3, fail_c3)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # ---- C4 odd-cycle control per odd-control record.
    stdout|     pass_c4, fail_c4 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("odd-control", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p) or "target_n" not in p:
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         n_odd = p["target_n"]
    stdout|         if not _is_number(n_odd) or int(n_odd) != E3_ODD_N:
    stdout|             diagnostics.append("C4: unsupported odd-control size %s (expect %s)" % (n_odd, E3_ODD_N))
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         kdim = _kernel_dim(E3_ODD_PAIRS, E3_ODD_N)
    stdout|         ref = {"node": nd.get("id"), "field": "target_n", "kernel_dim": kdim}
    stdout|         if kdim == 0:
    stdout|             pass_c4.append(ref)
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c4.append(ref)
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C4", "emits_no_nonzero_observable",
    stdout|                  "odd_cycle_false_invariant", pass_c4, fail_c4)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # ---- C5 removal per generation record.
    stdout|     pass_c5, fail_c5 = [], []
    stdout|     kv = _kernel_vector(E3_MOVE_PAIRS, E3_N)
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("generation", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         on_cand = p.get("on_candidate")
    stdout|         off_cand = p.get("off_candidate")
    stdout|         if not _is_array(on_cand):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         on_ok = kv is not None and _same_line([x for x in on_cand], [x for x in kv])
    stdout|         ref = {"node": nd.get("id"), "field": "on_candidate"}
    stdout|         if on_ok and (off_cand is None or off_cand == []):
    stdout|             pass_c5.append(ref)
    stdout|         elif on_ok and off_cand not in (None, []):
    stdout|             fail_c5.append(ref)
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c5.append(ref)
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C5", "generation_disabled_removes_candidate",
    stdout|                  "candidate_survives_removal", pass_c5, fail_c5)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # ---- C6 no constant injection per weight provenance.
    stdout|     pass_c6, fail_c6 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in _weight_nodes(by_role):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         vec = p.get("vector")
    stdout|         prov = p.get("provenance")
    stdout|         if not _is_array(vec) or not _is_str(prov):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         kv = _kernel_vector(E3_MOVE_PAIRS, E3_N)
    stdout|         if prov == "derived" and kv is not None and _same_line([x for x in vec], [x for x in kv]):
    stdout|             pass_c6.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "field": "provenance"})
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c6.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "field": "provenance"})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C6", "weights_derived",
    stdout|                  "weights_injected", pass_c6, fail_c6)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     return evidence, citations, diagnostics
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- E4 descent
    stdout| E4_TWO_HOUSES = 2
    stdout| E4_MAX_DEGREE = 3
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _parse_e4_move(mv):
    stdout|     """Parse one E4 move record -> (e_in, e_out) or None.
    stdout| 
    stdout|     Supported record shapes (the contract uses e_in = enemies in own house, e_out = enemies in
    stdout|     the other house):
    stdout|       {e_in: int, e_out: int}
    stdout|       {d: degree, s: same-house}  where degree = e_in + e_out (so e_in = s, e_out = d - s)
    stdout|     """
    stdout|     if not _is_mapping(mv):
    stdout|         return None
    stdout|     if "e_in" in mv and "e_out" in mv and _is_number(mv["e_in"]) and _is_number(mv["e_out"]):
    stdout|         return (int(mv["e_in"]), int(mv["e_out"]))
    stdout|     if "d" in mv and "s" in mv and _is_number(mv["d"]) and _is_number(mv["s"]):
    stdout|         d = int(mv["d"])
    stdout|         s = int(mv["s"])
    stdout|         return (s, d - s)
    stdout|     return None
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _read_int_field(payload, field, default=None):
    stdout|     if not _is_mapping(payload):
    stdout|         return default
    stdout|     if field not in payload:
    stdout|         return default
    stdout|     if not _is_number(payload[field]):
    stdout|         return default
    stdout|     return int(payload[field])
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _checked_e4(bundle, status):
    stdout|     """Derive E4 evidence: two-house partition, max degree <= 3, descent ΔH <= -1, bounded below."""
    stdout|     evidence = {c: {} for c in CHECKS}
    stdout|     citations = {}
    stdout|     diagnostics = []
    stdout|     by_role = _support_by_role(bundle, status)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- family-level facts from a "params"/"hypothesis" record.
    stdout|     houses = None
    stdout|     degree_bound = None
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("params", []) + by_role.get("hypothesis", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         if houses is None:
    stdout|             houses = _read_int_field(p, "houses")
    stdout|         if degree_bound is None:
    stdout|             degree_bound = _read_int_field(p, "degree_bound")
    stdout|     if houses is None:
    stdout|         diagnostics.append("E4: no houses parameter")
    stdout|     if degree_bound is None:
    stdout|         diagnostics.append("E4: no degree_bound parameter")
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C1 hypothesis: max degree <= 3 (load-bearing).
    stdout|     # A FAIL bit (argues_from_e_in_alone) is set ONLY when a descent argument is present but the
    stdout|     # degree bound is absent. Pure absence (no degree bound AND no descent argument) stays CD.
    stdout|     pass_c1, fail_c1 = [], []
    stdout|     descent_argued = any(True for nd in by_role.get("moves", [])
    stdout|                          if _is_mapping(nd.get("payload")))
    stdout|     if degree_bound is not None:
    stdout|         if degree_bound <= E4_MAX_DEGREE:
    stdout|             pass_c1.append({"field": "degree_bound", "value": degree_bound})
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c1.append({"field": "degree_bound", "value": degree_bound})
    stdout|     elif descent_argued:
    stdout|         # descent argued from e_in >= 2 alone, no degree bound
    stdout|         fail_c1.append({"field": "degree_bound", "value": None})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C1", "cites_max_degree_le_3",
    stdout|                  "argues_from_e_in_alone", pass_c1, fail_c1)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C2 exactly two houses.
    stdout|     pass_c2, fail_c2 = [], []
    stdout|     if houses is not None:
    stdout|         if houses == E4_TWO_HOUSES:
    stdout|             pass_c2.append({"field": "houses", "value": houses})
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c2.append({"field": "houses", "value": houses})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C2", "states_exactly_two_houses",
    stdout|                  "house_target_ambiguous", pass_c2, fail_c2)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C3 descent: for every legal move, ΔH = e_out - e_in <= -1.
    stdout|     pass_c3, fail_c3 = [], []
    stdout|     seen_move = False
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("moves", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         mvs = p.get("moves")
    stdout|         if not _is_array(mvs):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         seen_move = True
    stdout|         bad = False
    stdout|         for mv in mvs:
    stdout|             parsed = _parse_e4_move(mv)
    stdout|             if parsed is None:
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             e_in, e_out = parsed
    stdout|             # legality condition: e_in >= 2 (member has >= 2 enemies in own house)
    stdout|             if not (e_in >= 2):
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             delta = e_out - e_in
    stdout|             # A move is only "descent" if the user is in own-house >= 2 AND degree bound holds.
    stdout|             # The pinned bound e_in + e_out <= 3, so the legal descent gives delta <= -1.
    stdout|             if (e_in + e_out) > E4_MAX_DEGREE:
    stdout|                 # violates degree bound: not a legal descent move -> reject as descent proof
    stdout|                 fail_c3.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "e_in": e_in, "e_out": e_out,
    stdout|                                 "reason": "degree-bound-violated"})
    stdout|                 bad = True
    stdout|                 break
    stdout|             if delta > -1:
    stdout|                 fail_c3.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "e_in": e_in, "e_out": e_out,
    stdout|                                 "delta": delta})
    stdout|                 bad = True
    stdout|                 break
    stdout|         if not bad:
    stdout|             pass_c3.append({"node": nd.get("id")})
    stdout|     if not seen_move:
    stdout|         diagnostics.append("E4: no move record to check descent")
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C3", "derives_delta_h_le_minus1",
    stdout|                  "asserts_unbounded_descent", pass_c3, fail_c3)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C4 well-founded: H bounded below by 0.
    stdout|     pass_c4, fail_c4 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("bound", []) + by_role.get("wellfounded", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         lb = _read_int_field(p, "lower_bound")
    stdout|         if lb is not None and lb >= 0:
    stdout|             pass_c4.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "lower_bound": lb})
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c4.append({"node": nd.get("id")})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C4", "notes_h_bounded_below",
    stdout|                  "termination_no_lower_bound", pass_c4, fail_c4)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C5 termination != global min.
    stdout|     pass_c5, fail_c5 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("terminal", []) + by_role.get("terminus", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         claim = p.get("claim")
    stdout|         if claim == "no_legal_move":
    stdout|             pass_c5.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "claim": claim})
    stdout|         elif claim == "global_min":
    stdout|             fail_c5.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "claim": claim})
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             # if only the bound was claimed, no statement about min -> CD (no bit)
    stdout|             pass
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C5", "termination_not_global_min",
    stdout|                  "concludes_global_minimum", pass_c5, fail_c5)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C6 no constant injection: measure derived, not supplied.
    stdout|     pass_c6, fail_c6 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("measure", []) + by_role.get("monovariant", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         prov = p.get("provenance")
    stdout|         if prov == "derived":
    stdout|             pass_c6.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "provenance": prov})
    stdout|         elif prov == "supplied":
    stdout|             fail_c6.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "provenance": prov})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C6", "measure_derived",
    stdout|                  "measure_injected", pass_c6, fail_c6)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     return evidence, citations, diagnostics
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- E7 modular invariance
    stdout| E7_WINDOW = 4
    stdout| E7_MODULUS = 4
    stdout| E7_ODD_WINDOW = 3
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _window_product(w, n, start, width):
    stdout|     prod = 1
    stdout|     for k in range(width):
    stdout|         prod *= w[(start + k) % n]
    stdout|     return prod
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _sum_products_mod(w, n, width, modulus):
    stdout|     total = 0
    stdout|     for start in range(n):
    stdout|         total += _window_product(w, n, start, width)
    stdout|     return total % modulus
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _flip_delta_mod(w, n, pos, width, modulus):
    stdout|     """Change in the sum-of-products mod modulus when sign of w[pos] is flipped."""
    stdout|     before = _sum_products_mod(w, n, width, modulus)
    stdout|     b = list(w)
    stdout|     b[pos] = -b[pos]
    stdout|     after = _sum_products_mod(b, n, width, modulus)
    stdout|     return (after - before) % modulus
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _checked_e7(bundle, status):
    stdout|     """Derive E7 evidence: width-4 mod-4 preservation, separation, width-3 negative control."""
    stdout|     evidence = {c: {} for c in CHECKS}
    stdout|     citations = {}
    stdout|     diagnostics = []
    stdout|     by_role = _support_by_role(bundle, status)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- family params.
    stdout|     window_width = None
    stdout|     modulus = None
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("params", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         if window_width is None:
    stdout|             window_width = _read_int_field(p, "window_width")
    stdout|         if modulus is None:
    stdout|             modulus = _read_int_field(p, "modulus")
    stdout|     if window_width is None:
    stdout|         diagnostics.append("E7: no window_width parameter")
    stdout|     if modulus is None:
    stdout|         diagnostics.append("E7: no modulus parameter")
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C1 derived from flip-sign move set: a width-4 observable actually derived.
    stdout|     # PASS requires BOTH the width-4 structure AND a derived-observable node (an explicit
    stdout|     # derivation record). A bare window_width tag alone is not a derivation — that is the
    stdout|     # "tag never establishes evidence" discipline, same as E3/E4. A FAIL bit is set only when an
    stdout|     # observable is present but the width-4 move set / derivation is absent. Pure absence stays CD.
    stdout|     pass_c1, fail_c1 = [], []
    stdout|     obs_nodes = by_role.get("observable", []) + by_role.get("measure", [])
    stdout|     observable_present = any(_is_mapping(nd.get("payload")) for nd in obs_nodes)
    stdout|     derived_obs = any(_is_mapping(nd.get("payload")) and nd.get("payload").get("provenance") == "derived"
    stdout|                       for nd in obs_nodes)
    stdout|     if window_width == E7_WINDOW and derived_obs:
    stdout|         pass_c1.append({"field": "window_width", "value": window_width, "derived": True})
    stdout|     elif window_width is not None and window_width != E7_WINDOW:
    stdout|         fail_c1.append({"field": "window_width", "value": window_width})
    stdout|     elif observable_present:
    stdout|         fail_c1.append({"field": "derived_observable", "value": derived_obs})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C1", "derived_from_flip_sign",
    stdout|                  "invented_without_move_set", pass_c1, fail_c1)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C2 preservation: ΔS ≡ 0 (mod 4) for every single flip in the samples.
    stdout|     pass_c2, fail_c2 = [], []
    stdout|     seen_sample = False
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("samples", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         seqs = p.get("sequences")
    stdout|         if not _is_array(seqs):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         for seq in seqs:
    stdout|             if not _is_array(seq):
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             if not all(_is_number(x) for x in seq):
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             n = len(seq)
    stdout|             wf = _read_int_field(p, "window_width")
    stdout|             width = wf if wf is not None else window_width
    stdout|             if width is None:
    stdout|                 diagnostics.append("E7: sample has no resolvable window_width")
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             if width != E7_WINDOW:
    stdout|                 # sample width mismatch -> reject as preservation proof
    stdout|                 fail_c2.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "width": width, "reason": "width-not-4"})
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             seen_sample = True
    stdout|             all_ok = True
    stdout|             for pos in range(n):
    stdout|                 if _flip_delta_mod(list(seq), n, pos, width, E7_MODULUS) != 0:
    stdout|                     all_ok = False
    stdout|                     break
    stdout|             if all_ok:
    stdout|                 pass_c2.append({"node": nd.get("id")})
    stdout|             else:
    stdout|                 fail_c2.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "reason": "residue-changed"})
    stdout|     if not seen_sample:
    stdout|         diagnostics.append("E7: no valid width-4 sample sequence to check preservation")
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C2", "shows_even_window_delta",
    stdout|                  "preservation_no_even_argument", pass_c2, fail_c2)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C3 separation: start residue differs from target whenever 4 does not divide n.
    stdout|     pass_c3, fail_c3 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("separate", []) + by_role.get("separation", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         start_res = _read_int_field(p, "start_residue")
    stdout|         target_res = _read_int_field(p, "target_residue")
    stdout|         n = _read_int_field(p, "n")
    stdout|         if target_res is not None and n is not None and (n % E7_MODULUS) != 0:
    stdout|             # 4 ∤ n -> residues differ => separation holds
    stdout|             if target_res != 0:
    stdout|                 pass_c3.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "n": n, "target_residue": target_res})
    stdout|             else:
    stdout|                 fail_c3.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "n": n, "target_residue": target_res})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C3", "start_residue_differs",
    stdout|                  "cannot_separate_4_divides", pass_c3, fail_c3)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C4 negative control: width-3 emits ΔS ≡ 2 (mod 4).
    stdout|     pass_c4, fail_c4 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("control", []) + by_role.get("odd-control", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         seq = p.get("sample")
    stdout|         if not _is_array(seq) or not all(_is_number(x) for x in seq):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         n = len(seq)
    stdout|         deltas = set()
    stdout|         for pos in range(n):
    stdout|             deltas.add(_flip_delta_mod(list(seq), n, pos, E7_ODD_WINDOW, E7_MODULUS))
    stdout|         if deltas == {2}:
    stdout|             pass_c4.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "width": E7_ODD_WINDOW, "deltas": sorted(deltas)})
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             fail_c4.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "width": E7_ODD_WINDOW, "deltas": sorted(deltas)})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C4", "emits_odd_width_control",
    stdout|                  "omits_odd_width_control", pass_c4, fail_c4)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C5 rejected candidates classified.
    stdout|     pass_c5, fail_c5 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("rejected", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         candidates = p.get("candidates")
    stdout|         if not _is_array(candidates):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         bad = False
    stdout|         for cand in candidates:
    stdout|             if not _is_mapping(cand):
    stdout|                 continue
    stdout|             name = cand.get("name")
    stdout|             cls = cand.get("class")
    stdout|             if cls == "not_invariant" or cls == "preserved_but_non_separating":
    stdout|                 pass_c5.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "name": name, "class": cls})
    stdout|             elif cls == "invariant" or cls == "separating":
    stdout|                 fail_c5.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "name": name, "class": cls})
    stdout|                 bad = True
    stdout|                 break
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C5", "classifies_rejected",
    stdout|                  "misclassifies_rejected", pass_c5, fail_c5)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- C6 no constant injection: observables derived, not supplied.
    stdout|     pass_c6, fail_c6 = [], []
    stdout|     for nd in by_role.get("observable", []) + by_role.get("measure", []):
    stdout|         p = nd.get("payload")
    stdout|         if not _is_mapping(p):
    stdout|             continue
    stdout|         prov = p.get("provenance")
    stdout|         if prov == "derived":
    stdout|             pass_c6.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "provenance": prov})
    stdout|         elif prov == "supplied":
    stdout|             fail_c6.append({"node": nd.get("id"), "provenance": prov})
    stdout|     _combine_set(evidence, citations, "C6", "observable_derived",
    stdout|                  "weights_injected", pass_c6, fail_c6)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     return evidence, citations, diagnostics
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- identity
    stdout| def _sha256_text(text):
    stdout|     return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _bundle_digest(bundle):
    stdout|     cannon = json.dumps(bundle, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    stdout|     return _sha256_text(cannon)
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _extractor_identity():
    stdout|     here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    stdout|     path = os.path.join(here, os.path.basename(__file__))
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout|             return _sha256_text(f.read())
    stdout|     except OSError:
    stdout|         return "unavailable"
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _identity_block(bundle):
    stdout|     contract = bundle.get("contract")
    stdout|     return {
    stdout|         "bundle_digest": _bundle_digest(bundle),
    stdout|         "extractor": "cur_extract_evidence.py",
    stdout|         "extractor_version": EXTRACTOR_VERSION,
    stdout|         "extractor_digest": _extractor_identity(),
    stdout|         "grader_ruleset_id": RULESET_ID,
    stdout|         "grader_schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
    stdout|         "contract_ref": PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS.get(contract),
    stdout|         "contract_ref_commit": PINNED_CONTRACT_COMMITS.get(contract),
    stdout|         "contract_ref_content_digest": PINNED_CONTRACT_DIGESTS.get(contract),
    stdout|         "contract_in_tree_path": PINNED_CONTRACT_IN_TREE.get(contract),
    stdout|         "contract_in_tree_content_digest": PINNED_CONTRACT_DIGESTS.get(contract),
    stdout|         "rubric_ref": PINNED_RUBRIC_REF,
    stdout|         "rubric_ref_commit": PINNED_RUBRIC_COMMIT,
    stdout|         "rubric_ref_content_digest": PINNED_RUBRIC_DIGEST,
    stdout|     }
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- manifest
    stdout| def extract_bundle(bundle):
    stdout|     """Return (evidence, citations, proof_diags, handler_diags, broken_count)."""
    stdout|     _validate_bundle(bundle)
    stdout|     contract = bundle["contract"]
    stdout|     status, proof_diags = _resolve_proof(bundle)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     if contract == "E3":
    stdout|         evidence, citations, handler_diags = _checked_e3(bundle, status)
    stdout|     elif contract == "E4":
    stdout|         evidence, citations, handler_diags = _checked_e4(bundle, status)
    stdout|     elif contract == "E7":
    stdout|         evidence, citations, handler_diags = _checked_e7(bundle, status)
    stdout|     else:
    stdout|         evidence = {c: {} for c in CHECKS}
    stdout|         citations = {}
    stdout|         handler_diags = ["no checked evidence handler for contract %s; all checks CANNOT_DETERMINE (role names do not establish PASS)" % (contract,)]
    stdout| 
    stdout|     broken_count = len(proof_diags)
    stdout|     return evidence, citations, proof_diags, handler_diags, broken_count
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def manifest_from_bundle(bundle):
    stdout|     """Build the complete evidence manifest dict for a parsed bundle."""
    stdout|     contract = bundle.get("contract")
    stdout|     evidence, citations, proof_diags, handler_diags, broken_count = extract_bundle(bundle)
    stdout|     manifest = {
    stdout|         "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
    stdout|         "ruleset_id": RULESET_ID,
    stdout|         "contract": contract,
    stdout|         "contract_ref": PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS.get(contract),
    stdout|         "rubric_ref": PINNED_RUBRIC_REF,
    stdout|         "evidence": evidence,
    stdout|         "extractor": "cur_extract_evidence.py",
    stdout|         "extractor_schema": BUNDLE_SCHEMA,
    stdout|         "extractor_version": EXTRACTOR_VERSION,
    stdout|         "extractor_diagnostics": proof_diags,
    stdout|         "extractor_handler_diagnostics": handler_diags,
    stdout|         "citations": citations,
    stdout|         "identity": _identity_block(bundle),
    stdout|     }
    stdout|     return manifest, broken_count
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- CLI
    stdout| def main(argv):
    stdout|     if len(argv) < 2:
    stdout|         print("usage: cur_extract_evidence.py <bundle.json|bundle_dir> [--out <manifest.json>]", file=sys.stderr)
    stdout|         return 2
    stdout|     src = argv[1]
    stdout|     out_path = None
    stdout|     i = 2
    stdout|     while i < len(argv):
    stdout|         if argv[i] == "--out" and i + 1 < len(argv):
    stdout|             out_path = argv[i + 1]
    stdout|             i += 2
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             i += 1
    stdout| 
    stdout|     if os.path.isdir(src):
    stdout|         jsons = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(src) if f.endswith(".json"))
    stdout|         if len(jsons) != 1:
    stdout|             return _emit_error("directory must contain exactly one bundle json")
    stdout|         src = os.path.join(src, jsons[0])
    stdout| 
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         with open(src, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout|             bundle = json.load(f)
    stdout|     except OSError as e:
    stdout|         return _emit_error("cannot read bundle: %s" % (e,))
    stdout|     except ValueError as e:
    stdout|         return _emit_error("invalid JSON: %s" % (e,))
    stdout| 
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         manifest, broken_count = manifest_from_bundle(bundle)
    stdout|     except ValueError as e:
    stdout|         return _emit_error(str(e))
    stdout| 
    stdout|     text = json.dumps(manifest)
    stdout|     if out_path:
    stdout|         try:
    stdout|             with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout|                 f.write(text)
    stdout|         except OSError as e:
    stdout|             return _emit_error("cannot write manifest: %s" % (e,))
    stdout|     else:
    stdout|         print(text)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     return 1 if broken_count > 0 else 0
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _emit_error(msg):
    stdout|     print(json.dumps({"error": msg, "final": "MALFORMED", "discrepancy": "none"}))
    stdout|     return 2
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| if __name__ == "__main__":
    stdout|     sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

    stdout| #!/usr/bin/env python3
    stdout| """cur_grade_artifact.py — strict executable evaluator for the E3/E4/E7 grader contracts.
    stdout| 
    stdout| Consumes a *structured evidence manifest* (JSON) and derives, per check C1..C6, a verdict of
    stdout| PASS / FAIL / CANNOT_DETERMINE from the manifest's **evidence fields** — never from any
    stdout| expected/discrepancy label the manifest might carry.
    stdout| 
    stdout| SCOPE / HOST TOOL BOUNDARY
    stdout| --------------------------
    stdout| This is host-side tooling under tools/. It does NOT hold machine values, does not construct
    stdout| machine terms, and is NOT part of the machine runtime (core.py / labels.py / packs). The standing
    stdout| constraints on machine-native code (no Python lists/dicts/bools AS MACHINE VALUES; no isinstance
    stdout| /type/getattr/callable in the ENGINE) govern the machine implementation, not this evaluator. To
    stdout| keep the boundary unambiguous this file deliberately avoids isinstance/type/getattr/callable and
    stdout| uses only identity / attribute-availability checks and json-native values. It is NOT machine code.
    stdout| It never installs or fires a machine rule.
    stdout| 
    stdout| Scope prohibitions (unchanged): no machine implementation files, no oracle-card edits, no
    stdout| planner/search/matcher changes, no packs, no TrainingRecords, no target sessions, no tag cuts.
    stdout| 
    stdout| STRICTNESS (this hardening batch)
    stdout| ---------------------------------
    stdout| * Every evidence value must be a JSON boolean exactly (true/false). A non-boolean value
    stdout|   (string, number, array, object, null) -> exit 2.
    stdout| * Every evidence check object must only contain KNOWN keys (the pass/fail keys for that
    stdout|   contract/check). An unknown key -> exit 2 (a misspelled key must not silently become
    stdout|   CANNOT_DETERMINE).
    stdout| * A check whose evidence satisfies BOTH a pass rule and a fail rule -> exit 2 (contradictory).
    stdout| * Unsupported contract or check -> exit 2.
    stdout| * Multi-taxonomy failures (two or more distinct discrepancy labels) -> exit 2 AMBIGUOUS_DISCREPANCY;
    stdout|   never pick one by iteration order.
    stdout| * Ruleset identity: the manifest must carry the matching schema_version, ruleset_id, and
    stdout|   pinned contract/rubric identities, else EXIT 2.
    stdout| 
    stdout| Exit codes:
    stdout|   0 all six checks PASS
    stdout|   1 one or more checks FAIL (exactly one discrepancy label)
    stdout|   2 malformed / contradictory / ambiguous / schema / ruleset mismatch
    stdout|   3 no failed checks, but at least one CANNOT_DETERMINE
    stdout| 
    stdout| The grader derives each verdict from evidence; it contains NO expected-label table and ignores any
    stdout| provided expected/discrepancy label.
    stdout| """
    stdout| 
    stdout| import hashlib
    stdout| import json
    stdout| import os
    stdout| import sys
    stdout| 
    stdout| CHECKS = ("C1", "C2", "C3", "C4", "C5", "C6")
    stdout| 
    stdout| SCHEMA_VERSION = 1
    stdout| 
    stdout| # Immutable rule tables (constants, not mutable machine state). Each per-check rule gives the
    stdout| # pass-evidence keys (ALL must be true -> PASS) and fail-evidence keys (ANY true -> FAIL).
    stdout| # These are compiled from CUR-GRADER-RUBRIC.md's per-contract PASS/FAIL columns.
    stdout| CONTRACT_EVIDENCE_RULES = {
    stdout|     "E4": {
    stdout|         "C1": (("cites_max_degree_le_3",), ("argues_from_e_in_alone",)),
    stdout|         "C2": (("states_exactly_two_houses",), ("house_target_ambiguous",)),
    stdout|         "C3": (("derives_delta_h_le_minus1",), ("asserts_unbounded_descent",)),
    stdout|         "C4": (("notes_h_bounded_below",), ("termination_no_lower_bound",)),
    stdout|         "C5": (("termination_not_global_min",), ("concludes_global_minimum",)),
    stdout|         "C6": (("measure_derived",), ("measure_injected",)),
    stdout|     },
    stdout|     "E7": {
    stdout|         "C1": (("derived_from_flip_sign",), ("invented_without_move_set",)),
    stdout|         "C2": (("shows_even_window_delta",), ("preservation_no_even_argument",)),
    stdout|         "C3": (("start_residue_differs",), ("cannot_separate_4_divides",)),
    stdout|         "C4": (("emits_odd_width_control",), ("omits_odd_width_control",)),
    stdout|         "C5": (("classifies_rejected",), ("misclassifies_rejected",)),
    stdout|         "C6": (("observable_derived",), ("weights_injected",)),
    stdout|     },
    stdout|     "E3": {
    stdout|         "C1": (("derived_from_move_constraints",), ("stated_without_move_family",)),
    stdout|         "C2": (("preserved_all_moves",), ("a_move_changes_reading",)),
    stdout|         "C3": (("start_differs_from_target",), ("start_equals_target",)),
    stdout|         "C4": (("emits_no_nonzero_observable",), ("odd_cycle_false_invariant",)),
    stdout|         "C5": (("generation_disabled_removes_candidate",), ("candidate_survives_removal",)),
    stdout|         "C6": (("weights_derived",), ("weights_injected",)),
    stdout|     },
    stdout| }
    stdout| 
    stdout| # Contract CHECK->TAXONOMY label maps (from the contracts' section-5 taxonomies + E3 rows).
    stdout| CONTRACT_TAXONOMY = {
    stdout|     "E4": (
    stdout|         (("C1", "C2"), "hypothesis-omitted"),
    stdout|         (("C3", "C4"), "descent-unsupported"),
    stdout|         (("C5",), "overclaim"),
    stdout|         (("C6",), "constant-injection"),
    stdout|     ),
    stdout|     "E7": (
    stdout|         (("C1",), "derivation-absent"),
    stdout|         (("C2", "C4"), "preservation-unsupported"),
    stdout|         (("C3", "C5"), "overclaim"),
    stdout|         (("C6",), "constant-injection"),
    stdout|     ),
    stdout|     "E3": (
    stdout|         (("C1",), "derivation-absent"),
    stdout|         (("C2",), "preservation-unsupported"),
    stdout|         (("C3", "C4"), "overclaim"),
    stdout|         (("C5", "C6"), "constant-injection"),
    stdout|     ),
    stdout| }
    stdout| 
    stdout| # Pinned identities of the inputs the ruleset was derived from.
    stdout| PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS = {
    stdout|     "E3": "CUR-ENGEL-E3-EVALUATOR-CONTRACT.md",
    stdout|     "E4": "CUR-ENGEL-E4-GRADER-CONTRACT.md",
    stdout|     "E7": "CUR-ENGEL-E7-GRADER-CONTRACT.md",
    stdout| }
    stdout| PINNED_RUBRIC_REF = "CUR-GRADER-RUBRIC.md"
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _ruleset_digest():
    stdout|     h = hashlib.sha256()
    stdout|     h.update(SCHEMA_VERSION.to_bytes(4, "big"))
    stdout|     for contract in sorted(CONTRACT_EVIDENCE_RULES):
    stdout|         for ck in sorted(CONTRACT_EVIDENCE_RULES[contract]):
    stdout|             for group in ("pass", "fail"):
    stdout|                 for key in CONTRACT_EVIDENCE_RULES[contract][ck][0 if group == "pass" else 1]:
    stdout|                     h.update(key.encode("utf-8"))
    stdout|                     h.update(b"\x00")
    stdout|     for contract in sorted(CONTRACT_TAXONOMY):
    stdout|         for checks, label in CONTRACT_TAXONOMY[contract]:
    stdout|             for c in sorted(checks):
    stdout|                 h.update(c.encode("utf-8"))
    stdout|             h.update(label.encode("utf-8"))
    stdout|             h.update(b"\x00")
    stdout|     return h.hexdigest()
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| RULESET_DIGEST = _ruleset_digest()
    stdout| RULESET_ID = "e3e4e7-grader-v%d-%s" % (SCHEMA_VERSION, RULESET_DIGEST[:16])
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # --------------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
    stdout| def _is_json_object(value):
    stdout|     """True iff value is a JSON object (a mapping). Uses attribute availability, not isinstance."""
    stdout|     if value is True or value is False or value is None:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     # A JSON object is the only json-native value that exposes .keys; a str/list/int does not.
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         value.keys
    stdout|     except AttributeError:
    stdout|         return False
    stdout|     # Reject non-mapping anything that happens to have .keys by a strict shape.
    stdout|     return True
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _is_json_boolean(value):
    stdout|     return value is True or value is False
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _reject_non_bool(value):
    stdout|     """True iff value is an acceptable evidence boolean (exact JSON true/false)."""
    stdout|     return _is_json_boolean(value)
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| # The artifact's key/value validation below needs no type-introspection for strings: a check's
    stdout| # evidence keys are validated by membership in the KNOWN-key set (which holds only strings), so a
    stdout| # non-string key simply fails membership. Values are validated as exact booleans. The artifact's
    stdout| # top-level contract/version/ref fields are validated by equality against known literals.
    stdout| 
    stdout| def validate_evidence(check_ev, contract, check):
    stdout|     """Validate one check's evidence object. Returns (ok, reason)."""
    stdout|     if not _is_json_object(check_ev):
    stdout|         return False, "check %s evidence is not a JSON object" % check
    stdout|     rules = CONTRACT_EVIDENCE_RULES[contract][check]
    stdout|     pass_keys, fail_keys = rules
    stdout|     known = set(pass_keys) | set(fail_keys)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # Reject unknown keys (a misspelled key must not silently become CANNOT_DETERMINE).
    stdout|     for k in check_ev:
    stdout|         if k not in known:
    stdout|             return False, "check %s unknown evidence key: %r" % (check, k)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # Values must be exactly booleans.
    stdout|     for k in check_ev:
    stdout|         if not _reject_non_bool(check_ev[k]):
    stdout|             return False, "check %s evidence %r is not a boolean" % (check, k)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # Contradictory: both a pass rule and a fail rule satisfied.
    stdout|     pass_true = all(bool(check_ev.get(k)) for k in pass_keys)
    stdout|     fail_true = any(bool(check_ev.get(k)) for k in fail_keys)
    stdout|     if pass_true and fail_true:
    stdout|         return False, "check %s contradictory evidence (both pass and fail)" % check
    stdout| 
    stdout|     return True, "ok"
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def grade(manifest):
    stdout|     """Grade one strict manifest.
    stdout| 
    stdout|     Returns (checks, final, discrepancy). Raises ValueError on malformed/contradictory/ambiguous
    stdout|     with a specific message so the CLI can map to exit 2.
    stdout|     """
    stdout|     if not _is_json_object(manifest):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("manifest must be a JSON object")
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- ruleset identity ---
    stdout|     if manifest.get("schema_version") != SCHEMA_VERSION:
    stdout|         raise ValueError("schema_version mismatch (got %r, want %d)" % (
    stdout|             manifest.get("schema_version"), SCHEMA_VERSION))
    stdout|     if manifest.get("ruleset_id") != RULESET_ID:
    stdout|         raise ValueError("ruleset_id mismatch (got %r, want %s)" % (
    stdout|             manifest.get("ruleset_id"), RULESET_ID))
    stdout|     if manifest.get("rubric_ref") is not None and manifest["rubric_ref"] != PINNED_RUBRIC_REF:
    stdout|         raise ValueError("rubric_ref mismatch: %r" % manifest.get("rubric_ref"))
    stdout| 
    stdout|     contract = manifest.get("contract")
    stdout|     if contract not in CONTRACT_EVIDENCE_RULES:
    stdout|         raise ValueError("unknown contract: %r (expected E3/E4/E7)" % (contract,))
    stdout|     if manifest.get("contract_ref") not in (None, PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS[contract]):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("contract_ref mismatch for %s" % contract)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     evidence = manifest.get("evidence")
    stdout|     if not _is_json_object(evidence):
    stdout|         raise ValueError("manifest must have an 'evidence' JSON object")
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- per-check validation + verdicts ---
    stdout|     verdicts = {}
    stdout|     failed_checks = []
    stdout|     cannot_determine = False
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # Reject missing/extra checks.
    stdout|     for c in CHECKS:
    stdout|         if c not in evidence:
    stdout|             raise ValueError("missing evidence for check %s" % c)
    stdout|         ev = evidence[c]
    stdout|         ok, reason = validate_evidence(ev, contract, c)
    stdout|         if not ok:
    stdout|             raise ValueError(reason)
    stdout| 
    stdout|     for c in CHECKS:
    stdout|         ev = evidence[c]
    stdout|         rules = CONTRACT_EVIDENCE_RULES[contract][c]
    stdout|         pass_keys, fail_keys = rules
    stdout|         # Use .get so an absent key (empty evidence object) counts as False, not a crash.
    stdout|         pass_true = all(bool(ev.get(k)) for k in pass_keys)
    stdout|         fail_true = any(bool(ev.get(k)) for k in fail_keys)
    stdout|         if fail_true:
    stdout|             verdicts[c] = "FAIL"
    stdout|             failed_checks.append(c)
    stdout|         elif pass_true:
    stdout|             verdicts[c] = "PASS"
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             verdicts[c] = "CANNOT_DETERMINE"
    stdout|             cannot_determine = True
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- final verdict ---
    stdout|     if failed_checks:
    stdout|         final = "FAIL"
    stdout|     elif cannot_determine:
    stdout|         final = "CANNOT_DETERMINE"
    stdout|     else:
    stdout|         final = "PASS"
    stdout| 
    stdout|     # --- discrepancy: collect ALL distinct labels; reject ambiguity ---
    stdout|     distinct_labels = set()
    stdout|     for checks, label in CONTRACT_TAXONOMY[contract]:
    stdout|         if set(checks) & set(failed_checks):
    stdout|             distinct_labels.add(label)
    stdout|     if len(distinct_labels) > 1:
    stdout|         raise ValueError("AMBIGUOUS_DISCREPANCY: %s" % ",".join(sorted(distinct_labels)))
    stdout|     discrepancy = next(iter(distinct_labels)) if distinct_labels else "none"
    stdout| 
    stdout|     return verdicts, final, discrepancy
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def main(argv):
    stdout|     if len(argv) != 2:
    stdout|         print("usage: cur_grade_artifact.py <manifest.json>", file=sys.stderr)
    stdout|         return 2
    stdout|     path = argv[1]
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout|             manifest = json.load(f)
    stdout|     except OSError as e:
    stdout|         print(json.dumps({"error": "cannot read manifest: %s" % e,
    stdout|                           "final": "MALFORMED", "discrepancy": "none"}))
    stdout|         return 2
    stdout|     except ValueError as e:  # JSONDecodeError subclasses ValueError
    stdout|         print(json.dumps({"error": "invalid JSON: %s" % e,
    stdout|                           "final": "MALFORMED", "discrepancy": "none"}))
    stdout|         return 2
    stdout| 
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         verdicts, final, discrepancy = grade(manifest)
    stdout|     except ValueError as e:
    stdout|         msg = str(e)
    stdout|         if msg.startswith("AMBIGUOUS_DISCREPANCY"):
    stdout|             print(json.dumps({"error": msg, "final": "AMBIGUOUS_DISCREPANCY",
    stdout|                               "discrepancy": "ambiguous"}))
    stdout|         else:
    stdout|             print(json.dumps({"error": msg, "final": "MALFORMED",
    stdout|                               "discrepancy": "none"}))
    stdout|         return 2
    stdout| 
    stdout|     out = {
    stdout|         "schema_version": manifest.get("schema_version"),
    stdout|         "contract": manifest.get("contract"),
    stdout|         "checks": verdicts,
    stdout|         "final": final,
    stdout|         "discrepancy": discrepancy,
    stdout|     }
    stdout|     print(json.dumps(out))
    stdout| 
    stdout|     if final == "FAIL":
    stdout|         return 1
    stdout|     if final == "CANNOT_DETERMINE":
    stdout|         return 3
    stdout|     return 0
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| if __name__ == "__main__":
    stdout|     sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

    stdout| #!/usr/bin/env python3
    stdout| """cur_pipeline.py — one-command entrypoint: frozen bundle -> evidence -> verdict.
    stdout| 
    stdout| Runs the full grader lane on a single frozen G-ENG-style bundle:
    stdout| 
    stdout|     bundle -> tools/cur_extract_evidence.py -> evidence manifest
    stdout|            -> tools/cur_grade_artifact.py -> C1..C6 verdicts
    stdout| 
    stdout| and emits a single combined transcript in which extractor diagnostics and grader
    stdout| results are kept as separate sections. It is pure glue over the two existing tools —
    stdout| no new policy, no re-implementation of the checked handlers or the grader ruleset.
    stdout| 
    stdout| SCOPE / HOST TOOL BOUNDARY
    stdout| --------------------------
    stdout| Host-side tooling under tools/, exactly like the extractor and grader. It does not
    stdout| construct machine values or machine terms and is not part of the machine runtime.
    stdout| It deliberately avoids isinstance/type/getattr/callable.
    stdout| 
    stdout| GATE RULES (hardening)
    stdout| ----------------------
    stdout| * Continuation is permitted ONLY when the extractor reports a documented status:
    stdout|   exit 0 (no proof debt) or exit 1 (proof debt). An unexpected exit, a signal, a
    stdout|   timeout, a missing manifest, or an invalid manifest identity is an EXECUTION ERROR
    stdout|   and is never graded.
    stdout| * Extraction always lands in a fresh, invocation-owned temporary directory. A manifest
    stdout|   left by an earlier run is never read as a grading input. The requested --out manifest
    stdout|   is published atomically only after extraction + identity validation succeed.
    stdout| * The manifest identity (bundle digest, extractor name + digest, schema version) is
    stdout|   validated against this invocation's input bundle and tools before grading.
    stdout| * Unknown CLI options and ambiguous bundle directories are rejected.
    stdout| * Outputs are published atomically and the input bundle is never overwritten.
    stdout| * Only invocation-owned temporary files are cleaned, including on failure paths.
    stdout| 
    stdout| USAGE
    stdout|   python3 tools/cur_pipeline.py <bundle.json|bundle_dir> \
    stdout|          [--out <manifest.json>] [--transcript <file>] [--timeout <seconds>]
    stdout| 
    stdout| ENV TEST SEAMS (host tooling only)
    stdout|   CUR_PIPELINE_EXTRACTOR  overrides the extractor command path (a python module).
    stdout|   CUR_PIPELINE_GRADER     overrides the grader command path (a python module).
    stdout| 
    stdout| EXIT
    stdout|   0  graded: final verdict PASS (all six checks PASS)
    stdout|   1  graded: final verdict FAIL (one or more checks FAIL)
    stdout|   2  either (a) a graded final verdict AMBIGUOUS / MALFORMED / schema / ruleset
    stdout|      mismatch, or (b) not graded: extractor rejected the bundle (exit 2), or
    stdout|      (c) not graded: EXECUTION ERROR (unexpected extractor/grader exit, signal,
    stdout|      timeout, invalid manifest, or output-write failure)
    stdout|   3  graded: no FAIL but at least one CANNOT_DETERMINE
    stdout| 
    stdout| The transcript always carries a PIPELINE_STATUS line that distinguishes an EXECUTION
    stdout| ERROR from a graded verdict, so a caller can tell "the tooling failed" from "the
    stdout| artifact failed." The numeric 0/1/2/3 interface is preserved; execution errors are
    stdout| reported separately and never converted into a PASS.
    stdout| """
    stdout|

    stdout| import sys

    stdout|

    stdout| def _sha256_text(text):
    stdout|     import hashlib
    stdout|     return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _read_text(path):
    stdout|     with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout|         return f.read()
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _bundle_digest_from_file(path):
    stdout|     """Reproduce the extractor's bundle digest: lexicographic JSON, compact separators."""
    stdout|     with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout|         bundle = json.load(f)
    stdout|     cannon = json.dumps(bundle, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    stdout|     return _sha256_text(cannon)
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _extractor_digest():
    stdout|     """sha256 of the extractor source as observed by a host consumer."""
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         return _sha256_text(_read_text(EXTRACTOR))
    stdout|     except OSError:
    stdout|         return "unavailable"
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _graded_verdict(stdout):
    stdout|     """Return the grader's verdict string if stdout carries a verdict object, else None."""
    stdout|     text = (stdout or "").strip()
    stdout|     if not text:
    stdout|         return None
    stdout|     tail = text.rsplit("\n", 1)[-1].strip()
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         obj = json.loads(tail)
    stdout|     except ValueError:
    stdout|         return None
    stdout|     final = obj.get("final")
    stdout|     if not isinstance(final, str):
    stdout|         return None
    stdout|     return final
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _atomic_write_text(path, text):
    stdout|     """Write text to a same-directory temp then atomically replace path. Never touches input."""
    stdout|     d = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path)) or "."
    stdout|     fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".cur_pipeline_pub_", dir=d)
    stdout|     try:
    stdout|         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout|             f.write(text)
    stdout|         os.replace(tmp, path)
    stdout|     except OSError:
    stdout|         try:
    stdout|             os.unlink(tmp)
    stdout|         except OSError:
    stdout|             pass
    stdout|         raise
    stdout| 
    stdout| 
    stdout| def _atomic_copy(src, dst):
    stdout|     """Copy src to dst atomically (content inspectable only after a clean replace)."""
    stdout|     text = _read_text(src)
    stdout|     _atomic_write_text(dst, text)
    stdout|

    stdout| def _emit(text):
    stdout|     print(text)

    stdout| 
    stdout|     # Resolve a bundle directory to a single json file; reject ambiguous directories.
    stdout|     if os.path.isdir(src):
