    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C4 CANNOT_DETERMINE (no lower bound claimed)",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "lower_bound = 0",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates integer/ordering",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {

    stdout| +        "shape": "string",
    stdout| +        "required": True,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C5",),
    stdout| +        "domain": "'no_legal_move' or 'global_min'",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C5 CANNOT_DETERMINE (no termination claim)",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "'no_legal_move'",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates membership; the global-min overclaim is the extractor's",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {
    stdout| +        "field": "measure.provenance",
    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",
    stdout| +        "roles": ["measure", "monovariant"],
    stdout| +        "contracts": ("E4",),
    stdout| +        "shape": "string",
    stdout| +        "required": True,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C6",),
    stdout| +        "domain": "'derived' or 'supplied'",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C6 CANNOT_DETERMINE (no measure provenance)",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "'derived'",
    stdout| +        "limit": "string equality only",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---------------- E7 payload fields ----------------
    stdout| +    {

    stdout| +        "canonical": "window_width = 4",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates integer/equality; derivation is the extractor's",

    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",

    stdout| +        "shape": "integer",
    stdout| +        "required": True,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C1", "C2", "C4"),
    stdout| +        "domain": "== E7_MODULUS (4)",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "residue arithmetic undetermined -> dependent checks CD",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "modulus = 4",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates integer/equality",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {
    stdout| +        "field": "samples.sequences",
    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",

    stdout| +        "shape": "array<array<number>>",
    stdout| +        "required": True,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C2",),
    stdout| +        "domain": "each sequence length >= declared window width (else window undefined)",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C2 CANNOT_DETERMINE (no sample sequence)",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "array of numeric sequences of length >= window_width",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates sequence shape + length-vs-width; residue delta computed by the extractor",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {
    stdout| +        "field": "samples.window_width",
    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",
    stdout| +        "roles": ["samples"],
    stdout| +        "contracts": ("E7",),
    stdout| +        "shape": "integer",
    stdout| +        "required": False,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C2",),
    stdout| +        "domain": ">= 1; per-record override of the family width",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "falls back to params.window_width",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "per-record width, or the family width when absent",
    stdout| +        "limit": "a non-canonical width is graded by the extractor (width-not-4 FAIL), not a schema reject",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {
    stdout| +        "field": "separation.target_residue",
    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",
    stdout| +        "roles": ["separate", "separation"],

    stdout| +        "canonical": "target_residue != 0 when 4 does not divide n",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates integer; the divide-4 separation is the extractor's",

    stdout| +        "checks": ("C3",),
    stdout| +        "domain": "integer residue",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C3 uses target residue + n when present",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "integer start residue",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates integer, not the residue relationship",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {
    stdout| +        "field": "separation.n",
    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",
    stdout| +        "roles": ["separate", "separation"],
    stdout| +        "contracts": ("E7",),
    stdout| +        "shape": "integer",
    stdout| +        "required": True,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C3",),
    stdout| +        "domain": "integer length; separation requires n % 4 != 0",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C3 CANNOT_DETERMINE (no length to divide)",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "n not divisible by 4",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates integer; divisibility is the extractor's",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {
    stdout| +        "field": "odd-control.sample",
    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",
    stdout| +        "roles": ["control", "odd-control"],
    stdout| +        "contracts": ("E7",),
    stdout| +        "shape": "array<number>",
    stdout| +        "required": True,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C4",),
    stdout| +        "domain": "numeric sequence; length >= E7_ODD_WINDOW (3)",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C4 CANNOT_DETERMINE (no odd-width control sample)",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "length->=3 numeric sequence",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates shape; the width-3 delta == {2} is computed by the extractor",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {
    stdout| +        "field": "rejected.candidates",
    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",
    stdout| +        "roles": ["rejected"],
    stdout| +        "contracts": ("E7",),
    stdout| +        "shape": "array<object>",
    stdout| +        "required": True,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C5",),
    stdout| +        "domain": "each has 'name' and 'class' in the allowed set",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C5 CANNOT_DETERMINE (no rejected-candidate classification)",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "class in {'not_invariant','preserved_but_non_separating','invariant','separating'}",
    stdout| +        "limit": "checker validates membership; the misclassification is the extractor's",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    {
    stdout| +        "field": "observable.provenance",
    stdout| +        "scope": "payload",
    stdout| +        "roles": ["observable", "measure"],
    stdout| +        "contracts": ("E7",),
    stdout| +        "shape": "string",
    stdout| +        "required": True,
    stdout| +        "checks": ("C1", "C6"),
    stdout| +        "domain": "'derived' or 'supplied'",
    stdout| +        "cd_when_absent": "C1/C6 CANNOT_DETERMINE (no derived observable)",
    stdout| +        "canonical": "'derived'",
    stdout| +        "limit": "string equality only",
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +]
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# Per-contract, per-check payload-requirement map used only to compute CHECKABLE_CONTENT_PRESENT
    stdout| +# metadata (never a verdict). A key is the checks label; value is a list of (roles, field) that a
    stdout| +# handler needs present to have something to compute on.
    stdout| +CHECKABLE_CONTENT = {
    stdout| +    "E3": {
    stdout| +        "C1": (("weights", "vector"), ("moves", "pairs")),
    stdout| +        "C2": (("weights", "vector"), ("moves", "pairs")),
    stdout| +        "C3": (("weights", "vector"), ("start", "vector")),
    stdout| +        "C4": (("odd-control", "target_n"),),
    stdout| +        "C5": (("generation", "on_candidate"),),
    stdout| +        "C6": (("weights", "provenance"),),
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    "E4": {
    stdout| +        "C1": (("params", "degree_bound"),),
    stdout| +        "C2": (("params", "houses"),),
    stdout| +        "C3": (("moves", "moves"),),
    stdout| +        "C4": (("bound", "lower_bound"),),
    stdout| +        "C5": (("terminal", "claim"),),
    stdout| +        "C6": (("measure", "provenance"),),
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +    "E7": {
    stdout| +        "C1": (("params", "window_width"), ("observable", "provenance")),
    stdout| +        "C2": (("samples", "sequences"),),
    stdout| +        "C3": (("separate", "target_residue"), ("separate", "n")),
    stdout| +        "C4": (("control", "sample"),),
    stdout| +        "C5": (("rejected", "candidates"),),
    stdout| +        "C6": (("observable", "provenance"),),
    stdout| +    },
    stdout| +}
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# Allowed classification strings for E7 rejected candidates.
    stdout| +E7_REJECTED_CLASSES = (
    stdout| +    "not_invariant",
    stdout| +    "preserved_but_non_separating",
    stdout| +    "invariant",
    stdout| +    "separating",
    stdout| +)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# --------------------------------------------------------------------- bundle traversal
    stdout| +def _iter_role_items(bundle):
    stdout| +    """Yield (id, role, payload, kind, cites) for every node and every record entry."""
    stdout| +    for nd in (bundle.get("nodes") or []):
    stdout| +        if not _is_mapping(nd):
    stdout| +            continue
    stdout| +        cite_field = nd.get("cites")
    stdout| +        if cite_field is None:
    stdout| +            cite_field = nd.get("refs")
    stdout| +        yield (nd.get("id"), nd.get("role"), nd.get("payload"),
    stdout| +               nd.get("kind"), cite_field)
    stdout| +    records = bundle.get("records")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(records):
    stdout| +        for rname, arr in records.items():
    stdout| +            if not _is_array(arr):
    stdout| +                continue
    stdout| +            for r in arr:
    stdout| +                if not _is_mapping(r):
    stdout| +                    continue
    stdout| +                cite_field = r.get("cites")
    stdout| +                if cite_field is None:
    stdout| +                    cite_field = r.get("refs")
    stdout| +                yield (r.get("id"), r.get("role"), r.get("payload"),
    stdout| +                       r.get("kind"), cite_field)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _roles_with_payload(bundle, contract):
    stdout| +    """Return {role: payload} for role items that carry a mapping payload."""
    stdout| +    out = {}
    stdout| +    for rid, role, payload, kind, cites in _iter_role_items(bundle):
    stdout| +        if not _is_str(role):
    stdout| +            continue
    stdout| +        if not _is_mapping(payload):
    stdout| +            continue
    stdout| +        if role not in out:
    stdout| +            out[role] = payload
    stdout| +    return out
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# --------------------------------------------------------------------- structural validation
    stdout| +def _validate_structure(bundle, contract, errors):
    stdout| +    """Structural checks that mirror the extractor's _validate_bundle, plus id uniqueness."""
    stdout| +    if not _is_mapping(bundle):
    stdout| +        errors.append("bundle must be a JSON object")
    stdout| +        return
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    if bundle.get("schema") != BUNDLE_SCHEMA:
    stdout| +        errors.append("unsupported bundle schema: %r" % (bundle.get("schema"),))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    if not _is_str(contract) or contract not in CONTRACT_SET:
    stdout| +        errors.append("unsupported contract: %r (expected E3/E4/E7)" % (contract,))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    cand = bundle.get("candidate")
    stdout| +    if not _is_mapping(cand):
    stdout| +        errors.append("bundle must have a 'candidate' object")
    stdout| +    elif not _is_str(cand.get("id")):
    stdout| +        errors.append("candidate must have a string 'id'")
    stdout| +    cand_cites = cand.get("cites") if _is_mapping(cand) else None
    stdout| +    if cand_cites is None and _is_mapping(cand):
    stdout| +        cand_cites = cand.get("refs")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(cand) and cand_cites is not None and not _is_array(cand_cites):
    stdout| +        errors.append("candidate.cites must be an array")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    claims = bundle.get("claims")
    stdout| +    if claims is not None and not _is_array(claims):
    stdout| +        errors.append("'claims' must be an array")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    nodes = bundle.get("nodes")
    stdout| +    if not _is_array(nodes):
    stdout| +        errors.append("'nodes' must be an array")
    stdout| +    else:
    stdout| +        for nd in nodes:
    stdout| +            if not _is_mapping(nd):
    stdout| +                errors.append("node must be an object")
    stdout| +                continue
    stdout| +            if not _is_str(nd.get("id")):
    stdout| +                errors.append("node must have a string 'id'")
    stdout| +            _check_cites_and_payload(nd, errors)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    records = bundle.get("records")
    stdout| +    if records is not None and not _is_mapping(records):
    stdout| +        errors.append("'records' must be an object")
    stdout| +    elif _is_mapping(records):
    stdout| +        for rname, arr in records.items():
    stdout| +            if not _is_array(arr):
    stdout| +                errors.append("records.%s must be an array" % (rname,))
    stdout| +                continue
    stdout| +            for r in arr:
    stdout| +                if not _is_mapping(r):
    stdout| +                    errors.append("records.%s entry must be an object" % (rname,))
    stdout| +                    continue
    stdout| +                _check_cites_and_payload(r, errors)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # id uniqueness across claims + candidate + nodes + records.
    stdout| +    seen = set()
    stdout| +    for c in (claims or []):
    stdout| +        if _is_mapping(c) and _is_str(c.get("id")):
    stdout| +            _note_id(c["id"], seen, errors)
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(cand) and _is_str(cand.get("id")):
    stdout| +        _note_id(cand["id"], seen, errors)
    stdout| +    for rid, role, payload, kind, cites in _iter_role_items(bundle):
    stdout| +        if _is_str(rid):
    stdout| +            _note_id(rid, seen, errors)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _check_cites_and_payload(obj, errors):
    stdout| +    cites = obj.get("cites")
    stdout| +    if cites is None:
    stdout| +        cites = obj.get("refs")
    stdout| +    if cites is not None and not _is_array(cites):
    stdout| +        errors.append("cites must be an array (node %s)" % (obj.get("id"),))
    stdout| +    elif _is_array(cites):
    stdout| +        for c in cites:
    stdout| +            if not _is_str(c):
    stdout| +                errors.append("cite must be a string id (node %s)" % (obj.get("id"),))
    stdout| +    if obj.get("payload") is not None and not _is_mapping(obj.get("payload")):
    stdout| +        errors.append("payload must be an object (node %s)" % (obj.get("id"),))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _note_id(oid, seen, errors):
    stdout| +    if oid in seen:
    stdout| +        errors.append("duplicate id %r" % (oid,))
    stdout| +    seen.add(oid)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _read_int(payload, key):
    stdout| +    if not _is_mapping(payload):
    stdout| +        return None
    stdout| +    v = payload.get(key)
    stdout| +    if not _is_number(v):
    stdout| +        return None
    stdout| +    return int(v)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# --------------------------------------------------------------------- contract-specific checks
    stdout| +def _validate_contract(contract, bundle, errors):
    stdout| +    roles = _roles_with_payload(bundle, contract)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    if contract == "E3":
    stdout| +        _validate_e3(roles, errors)
    stdout| +    elif contract == "E4":
    stdout| +        _validate_e4(roles, errors)
    stdout| +    elif contract == "E7":
    stdout| +        _validate_e7(roles, errors)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _validate_e3(roles, errors):
    stdout| +    w = roles.get("weights")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(w) and "vector" in w:
    stdout| +        vec = w["vector"]
    stdout| +        if not _is_array(vec) or not all(_is_number(x) for x in vec):
    stdout| +            errors.append("E3 weights.vector must be an array of numbers")
    stdout| +        elif len(vec) != E3_N:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E3 weights.vector length %s != pinned %s" % (len(vec), E3_N))
    stdout| +    m = roles.get("moves")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(m) and "pairs" in m:
    stdout| +        pairs = m["pairs"]
    stdout| +        if not _is_array(pairs):
    stdout| +            errors.append("E3 moves.pairs must be an array")
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            for p in pairs:
    stdout| +                if not (_is_array(p) and len(p) == 2 and _is_number(p[0]) and _is_number(p[1])):
    stdout| +                    errors.append("E3 moves.pairs entry must be [integer, integer]")
    stdout| +    s = roles.get("start")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(s) and "vector" in s:
    stdout| +        sv = s["vector"]
    stdout| +        if not _is_array(sv) or not all(_is_number(x) for x in sv):
    stdout| +            errors.append("E3 start.vector must be an array of numbers")
    stdout| +        elif len(sv) != E3_N:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E3 start.vector length %s != pinned %s" % (len(sv), E3_N))
    stdout| +    oc = roles.get("odd-control")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(oc) and "target_n" in oc:
    stdout| +        t = _read_int(oc, "target_n")
    stdout| +        if t is None:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E3 odd-control.target_n must be an integer")
    stdout| +        elif t != E3_ODD_N:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E3 odd-control.target_n %s != pinned %s" % (t, E3_ODD_N))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _validate_e4(roles, errors):
    stdout| +    # C1/C2 params.
    stdout| +    params = roles.get("params")
    stdout| +    if params is None:
    stdout| +        params = roles.get("hypothesis")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(params):
    stdout| +        if "houses" in params:
    stdout| +            h = _read_int(params, "houses")
    stdout| +            if h is None:
    stdout| +                errors.append("E4 params.houses must be an integer")
    stdout| +        if "degree_bound" in params:
    stdout| +            db = _read_int(params, "degree_bound")
    stdout| +            if db is None:
    stdout| +                errors.append("E4 params.degree_bound must be an integer")
    stdout| +            elif db > E4_MAX_DEGREE:
    stdout| +                errors.append("E4 params.degree_bound %s > pinned max %s" % (db, E4_MAX_DEGREE))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # C3 moves: dual-form equivalence, else reject.
    stdout| +    moves = roles.get("moves")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(moves) and "moves" in moves:
    stdout| +        arr = moves["moves"]
    stdout| +        if not _is_array(arr):
    stdout| +            errors.append("E4 moves.moves must be an array")
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            for mv in arr:
    stdout| +                if not _is_mapping(mv):
    stdout| +                    errors.append("E4 move must be an object")
    stdout| +                    continue
    stdout| +                has_e = ("e_in" in mv) and ("e_out" in mv)
    stdout| +                has_ds = ("d" in mv) and ("s" in mv)
    stdout| +                if not has_e and not has_ds:
    stdout| +                    errors.append("E4 move must have {e_in,e_out} or {d,s}")
    stdout| +                    continue
    stdout| +                e_in = _read_int(mv, "e_in")
    stdout| +                e_out = _read_int(mv, "e_out")
    stdout| +                d = _read_int(mv, "d")
    stdout| +                s = _read_int(mv, "s")
    stdout| +                if has_e and (e_in is None or e_out is None):
    stdout| +                    errors.append("E4 move e_in/e_out must be integers")
    stdout| +                if has_ds and (d is None or s is None):
    stdout| +                    errors.append("E4 move d/s must be integers")
    stdout| +                # dual-form contradiction check when both present.
    stdout| +                if has_e and has_ds:
    stdout| +                    if s != e_in or d != e_in + e_out:
    stdout| +                        errors.append(
    stdout| +                            "E4 move has contradictory e_in/e_out vs d/s "
    stdout| +                            "(s=%s e_in=%s d=%s sum=%s)" % (s, e_in, d, e_in + e_out))
    stdout| +                elif has_e:
    stdout| +                    if e_in < 0 or e_out < 0:
    stdout| +                        errors.append("E4 move e_in/e_out must be non-negative")
    stdout| +                elif has_ds:
    stdout| +                    if d < 0 or s < 0 or s > d:
    stdout| +                        errors.append("E4 move d/s invalid (0 <= s <= d)")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # C4 bound.
    stdout| +    bound = roles.get("bound")
    stdout| +    if bound is None:
    stdout| +        bound = roles.get("wellfounded")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(bound) and "lower_bound" in bound:
    stdout| +        lb = _read_int(bound, "lower_bound")
    stdout| +        if lb is None:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E4 bound.lower_bound must be an integer")
    stdout| +        elif lb < 0:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E4 bound.lower_bound must be >= 0")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # C5 terminal claim.
    stdout| +    terminal = roles.get("terminal")
    stdout| +    if terminal is None:
    stdout| +        terminal = roles.get("terminus")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(terminal) and "claim" in terminal:
    stdout| +        claim = terminal["claim"]
    stdout| +        if not _is_str(claim) or claim not in ("no_legal_move", "global_min"):
    stdout| +            errors.append("E4 terminal.claim must be 'no_legal_move' or 'global_min'")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _validate_e7(roles, errors):
    stdout| +    params = roles.get("params")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(params):
    stdout| +        ww = _read_int(params, "window_width")
    stdout| +        if "window_width" in params and ww is None:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E7 params.window_width must be an integer")
    stdout| +        elif ww is not None and ww != E7_WINDOW:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E7 params.window_width %s != pinned %s" % (ww, E7_WINDOW))
    stdout| +        md = _read_int(params, "modulus")
    stdout| +        if "modulus" in params and md is None:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E7 params.modulus must be an integer")
    stdout| +        elif md is not None and md != E7_MODULUS:
    stdout| +            errors.append("E7 params.modulus %s != pinned %s" % (md, E7_MODULUS))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # C2 samples: every sequence must be at least as long as its window width.
    stdout| +    samples = roles.get("samples")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(samples) and "sequences" in samples:
    stdout| +        seqs = samples["sequences"]
    stdout| +        if not _is_array(seqs):
    stdout| +            errors.append("E7 samples.sequences must be an array")
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            declared_width = _read_int(samples, "window_width")
    stdout| +            family_width = declared_width if declared_width is not None else E7_WINDOW
    stdout| +            for seq in seqs:
    stdout| +                if not _is_array(seq) or not all(_is_number(x) for x in seq):
    stdout| +                    errors.append("E7 sample sequence must be an array of numbers")
    stdout| +                    continue
    stdout| +                if len(seq) < family_width:
    stdout| +                    errors.append(
    stdout| +                        "E7 sample sequence length %s < declared window width %s" % (
    stdout| +                            len(seq), family_width))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # C3 separation.
    stdout| +    sep = roles.get("separate")
    stdout| +    if sep is None:
    stdout| +        sep = roles.get("separation")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(sep):
    stdout| +        for key in ("target_residue", "n"):
    stdout| +            if key in sep and _read_int(sep, key) is None:
    stdout| +                errors.append("E7 separation.%s must be an integer" % (key,))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # C4 odd-width control.
    stdout| +    control = roles.get("control")
    stdout| +    if control is None:
    stdout| +        control = roles.get("odd-control")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(control) and "sample" in control:
    stdout| +        smp = control["sample"]
    stdout| +        if not _is_array(smp) or not all(_is_number(x) for x in smp):
    stdout| +            errors.append("E7 odd-control.sample must be an array of numbers")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # C5 rejected classification.
    stdout| +    rejected = roles.get("rejected")
    stdout| +    if _is_mapping(rejected) and "candidates" in rejected:
    stdout| +        cands = rejected["candidates"]
    stdout| +        if not _is_array(cands):
    stdout| +            errors.append("E7 rejected.candidates must be an array")
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            for c in cands:
    stdout| +                if not _is_mapping(c):
    stdout| +                    errors.append("E7 rejected candidate must be an object")
    stdout| +                    continue
    stdout| +                cls = c.get("class")
    stdout| +                if not _is_str(cls) or cls not in E7_REJECTED_CLASSES:
    stdout| +                    errors.append("E7 rejected candidate class %r not in allowed set" % (cls,))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# --------------------------------------------------------------------- checkable-content metadata
    stdout| +def _compute_checkable(contract, bundle):
    stdout| +    """Return {check: bool} whether the handler's payload fields are present, per contract."""
    stdout| +    roles = _roles_with_payload(bundle, contract)
    stdout| +    needs = CHECKABLE_CONTENT.get(contract, {})
    stdout| +    out = {}
    stdout| +    for check in CHECKS:
    stdout| +        reqs = needs.get(check, ())
    stdout| +        present = True
    stdout| +        for role, field in reqs:
    stdout| +            if role not in roles:
    stdout| +                present = False
    stdout| +                break
    stdout| +            payload = roles[role]
    stdout| +            # For checks keyed on a single field, require it present.
    stdout| +            if field and field not in payload:
    stdout| +                present = False
    stdout| +                break
    stdout| +            # sequences / moves / candidates are required to be non-empty arrays.
    stdout| +            if field in ("sequences", "moves", "candidates"):
    stdout| +                val = payload.get(field)
    stdout| +                if not _is_array(val) or len(val) == 0:
    stdout| +                    present = False
    stdout| +                    break
    stdout| +        out[check] = present
    stdout| +    return out
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# --------------------------------------------------------------------- checker CLI
    stdout| +def check_bundle(bundle, contract_hint=None):
    stdout| +    """Return (ok, report). ok is True iff schema-valid; report is a JSON-safe dict."""
    stdout| +    errors = []
    stdout| +    contract = contract_hint if contract_hint is not None else bundle.get("contract")
    stdout| +    _validate_structure(bundle, contract, errors)
    stdout| +    if _is_str(contract) and contract in CONTRACT_SET:
    stdout| +        _validate_contract(contract, bundle, errors)
    stdout| +    checkable = _compute_checkable(contract, bundle) if _is_str(contract) and contract in CONTRACT_SET else {}
    stdout| +    report = {
    stdout| +        "schema_valid": len(errors) == 0,
    stdout| +        "contract": contract,
    stdout| +        "errors": errors,
    stdout| +        "states": {
    stdout| +            "SCHEMA_VALID": len(errors) == 0,
    stdout| +            "CHECKABLE_CONTENT_PRESENT": checkable,
    stdout| +            "CHECK_ESTABLISHED": False,
    stdout| +            "PROOF_REPLAYED": False,
    stdout| +        },
    stdout| +        "checkable_content": checkable,
    stdout| +    }
    stdout| +    return len(errors) == 0, report
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def main(argv):
    stdout| +    if len(argv) < 2:
    stdout| +        sys.stderr.write(_USAGE + "\n")
    stdout| +        return 2
    stdout| +    cmd = argv[1]
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    if cmd == "emit-json":
    stdout| +        sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(_build_schema_json(), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")))
    stdout| +        sys.stdout.write("\n")
    stdout| +        return 0
    stdout| +    if cmd == "emit-markdown":
    stdout| +        sys.stdout.write(_build_schema_markdown())
    stdout| +        return 0
    stdout| +    if cmd == "check":
    stdout| +        if len(argv) < 3:
    stdout| +            sys.stderr.write(_USAGE + "\n")
    stdout| +            return 2
    stdout| +        src = argv[2]
    stdout| +        if os.path.isdir(src):
    stdout| +            jsons = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(src) if f.endswith(".json"))
    stdout| +            if len(jsons) != 1:
    stdout| +                sys.stderr.write("schema: directory must contain exactly one bundle json\n")
    stdout| +                return 2
    stdout| +            src = os.path.join(src, jsons[0])
    stdout| +        try:
    stdout| +            with open(src, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +                bundle = json.load(f)
    stdout| +        except OSError as e:
    stdout| +            sys.stderr.write("schema: cannot read bundle: %s\n" % (e,))
    stdout| +            return 2
    stdout| +        except ValueError as e:
    stdout| +            sys.stderr.write("schema: invalid JSON: %s\n" % (e,))
    stdout| +            return 2
    stdout| +        ok, report = check_bundle(bundle)
    stdout| +        sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(report, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")))
    stdout| +        sys.stdout.write("\n")
    stdout| +        return 0 if ok else 2
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    sys.stderr.write(_USAGE + "\n")
    stdout| +    sys.stderr.write("schema: unknown command: %s\n" % (cmd,))
    stdout| +    return 2
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +_USAGE = (
    stdout| +    "usage: cur_artifact_schema.py <check <bundle.json|bundle_dir> | emit-json | emit-markdown>"
    stdout| +)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# --------------------------------------------------------------------- emitted schema (json + md)
    stdout| +def _build_schema_json():
    stdout| +    """Machine-readable schema, deterministic (no timestamps, sort_keys)."""
    stdout| +    contract_fields = {}
    stdout| +    for c in CONTRACT_SET:
    stdout| +        cfields = []
    stdout| +        for rec in FIELD_TABLE:
    stdout| +            if c in rec["contracts"]:
    stdout| +                cfields.append(rec)
    stdout| +        contract_fields[c] = cfields
    stdout| +    return {
    stdout| +        "spec": "G-ENG-ARTIFACT-SCHEMA",
    stdout| +        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
    stdout| +        "ruleset_id": RULESET_ID,
    stdout| +        "bundle_schema": BUNDLE_SCHEMA,
    stdout| +        "extractor_version": EXTRACTOR_VERSION,
    stdout| +        "contracts": list(CONTRACT_SET),
    stdout| +        "checks": list(CHECKS),
    stdout| +        "pinned": {
    stdout| +            "contract_refs": PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS,
    stdout| +            "contract_commits": PINNED_CONTRACT_COMMITS,
    stdout| +            "contract_digests": PINNED_CONTRACT_DIGESTS,
    stdout| +            "contract_in_tree": PINNED_CONTRACT_IN_TREE,
    stdout| +            "rubric_ref": PINNED_RUBRIC_REF,
    stdout| +            "rubric_commit": PINNED_RUBRIC_COMMIT,
    stdout| +            "rubric_digest": PINNED_RUBRIC_DIGEST,
    stdout| +        },
    stdout| +        "canonical_constants": {
    stdout| +            "E3_N": E3_N,
    stdout| +            "E3_ODD_N": E3_ODD_N,
    stdout| +            "E4_TWO_HOUSES": E4_TWO_HOUSES,
    stdout| +            "E4_MAX_DEGREE": E4_MAX_DEGREE,
    stdout| +            "E7_WINDOW": E7_WINDOW,
    stdout| +            "E7_MODULUS": E7_MODULUS,
    stdout| +            "E7_ODD_WINDOW": E7_ODD_WINDOW,
    stdout| +        },
    stdout| +        "capability_ceiling": {
    stdout| +            "SCHEMA_VALID": "structure holds",
    stdout| +            "CHECKABLE_CONTENT_PRESENT": "payload present (metadata only)",
    stdout| +            "CHECK_ESTABLISHED": "not established by this module",
    stdout| +            "PROOF_REPLAYED": "not supported (tier-2.5 lane)",
    stdout| +        },
    stdout| +        "states": ["SCHEMA_VALID", "CHECKABLE_CONTENT_PRESENT", "CHECK_ESTABLISHED", "PROOF_REPLAYED"],
    stdout| +        "fields": FIELD_TABLE,
    stdout| +        "contract_fields": contract_fields,
    stdout| +    }
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _build_schema_markdown():
    stdout| +    """Markdown spec, deterministic (no timestamps)."""
    stdout| +    L = []
    stdout| +    L.append("# G-ENG-ARTIFACT-SCHEMA\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("Generated from the single declarative field table in `tools/cur_artifact_schema.py`, "
    stdout| +             "which is derived from what `tools/cur_extract_evidence.py`'s checked handlers read. "
    stdout| +             "It is the contract an author must satisfy before submitting a frozen artifact to the "
    stdout| +             "grader lane. No timestamps are embedded in this generated output.\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("- **Agent:** SCHEMA-eng")
    stdout| +    L.append("- **Evaluator-side, training-visible: no.**")
    stdout| +    L.append("- **Schema version:** `%d` (pinned to the grader `SCHEMA_VERSION`)." % SCHEMA_VERSION)
    stdout| +    L.append("- **Ruleset id:** `%s`" % RULESET_ID)
    stdout| +    L.append("- **Bundle schema:** `%s`" % BUNDLE_SCHEMA)
    stdout| +    L.append("- **Extractor version:** `%s`" % EXTRACTOR_VERSION)
    stdout| +    L.append("")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    L.append("## Capability ceiling (stated plainly)\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("This lane validates **checked, fixed-contract math**:\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("```text")
    stdout| +    L.append("frozen bundle -> cur_extract_evidence.py -> evidence manifest -> cur_grade_artifact.py")
    stdout| +    L.append("             -> C1..C6 verdicts")
    stdout| +    L.append("```")
    stdout| +    L.append("It is **tier-2.5**: it re-derives the pinned E3/E4/E7 facts (kernel, descent "
    stdout| +             "ΔH <= -1, mod-4 residue ΔS ≡ 0) by computation against the fixed problem. It does not "
    stdout| +             "read a bundle's self-declared verdict, and it does not tier-3 replay an arbitrary "
    stdout| +             "proof. A recognized role **never** establishes an evidence bit by itself — it only "
    stdout| +             "*selects* a handler.\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("The schema layer distinguishes four states and never conflates them:\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("| State | Meaning | Established by |")
    stdout| +    L.append("|---|---|---|")
    stdout| +    L.append("| `SCHEMA_VALID` | top-level shape, id uniqueness, citation arrays, collection shapes, numeric domains, and declared canonical move forms all hold | this module |")
    stdout| +    L.append("| `CHECKABLE_CONTENT_PRESENT` | the payload fields a check needs are present (metadata, never a verdict) | this module |")
    stdout| +    L.append("| `CHECK_ESTABLISHED` | an evidence bit set by actual computation against the pinned problem | the extractor's checked handlers |")
    stdout| +    L.append("| `PROOF_REPLAYED` | tier-3 full proof-checker replay of arbitrary cited nodes | **not supported** on this lane |")
    stdout| +    L.append("")
    stdout| +    L.append("**Schema validity never implies mathematical validity.** A bundle can be `SCHEMA_VALID` "
    stdout| +             "yet yield `CANNOT_DETERMINE` (or `FAIL`) downstream when its required payload is absent, "
    stdout| +             "empty, or unrelated.\n")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    L.append("## Global bundle shape\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("```json")
    stdout| +    L.append('{ "schema": "geng-bundle/v2", "contract": "E3 | E4 | E7",')
    stdout| +    L.append('  "candidate": { "id": "...", "role": "candidate", "cites": ["support-id", ...] },')
    stdout| +    L.append('  "claims": [ { "id": "...", "kind": "assumption", ... } ],')
    stdout| +    L.append('  "nodes": [ { "id": "...", "role": "...", "kind": "derived|assumption",')
    stdout| +    L.append('              "cites": ["support-id", ...], "payload": { ... } } ],')
    stdout| +    L.append('  "records": { "name": [ { "id": "...", "role": "...", "cites": [...], "payload": {...} } ] } }')
    stdout| +    L.append("```")
    stdout| +    L.append("**Required:** `schema` == `geng-bundle/v2`; `contract` in `{E3, E4, E7}`; a `candidate` "
    stdout| +             "object with a string `id`; `nodes` an array whose nodes have string `id`s, and (if "
    stdout| +             "present) string-array `cites`/`refs` and an object `payload`. **IDs must be unique** "
    stdout| +             "across `claims` + `candidate` + `nodes` + `records`.\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("Global malformations => `exit 2` (no manifest): duplicate id, non-array `cites`/"
    stdout| +             "`nodes`, non-object `payload`, unsupported schema, unsupported contract.\n")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    L.append("## Field definitions\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("Each record: field name (dotted), contract families, JSON shape, required/optional, "
    stdout| +             "numeric/domain constraint, checks fed, `CANNOT_DETERMINE`-when-absent effect, canonical "
    stdout| +             "representation, and the known validation limit of this schema layer.\n")
    stdout| +    for contract in CONTRACT_SET:
    stdout| +        L.append("### FIELDS `E%d` family\n" % _contract_num(contract))
    stdout| +        L.append("| field | contract | shape | required | domain | checks fed | CD-when-absent | canonical | validation limit |")
    stdout| +        L.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
    stdout| +        for rec in FIELD_TABLE:
    stdout| +            if contract in rec["contracts"]:
    stdout| +                L.append("| %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s | %s |" % (
    stdout| +                    _mdfield(rec["field"]),
    stdout| +                    ", ".join(rec["contracts"]),
    stdout| +                    rec["shape"],
    stdout| +                    "yes" if rec["required"] else "no",
    stdout| +                    rec["domain"],
    stdout| +                    ", ".join(rec["checks"]),
    stdout| +                    rec["cd_when_absent"],
    stdout| +                    rec["canonical"],
    stdout| +                    rec["limit"],
    stdout| +                ))
    stdout| +        L.append("")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    L.append("## Canonical move forms\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("- **E3:** exact integer weights (length `E3_N` = `%d`), complete declared move family "
    stdout| +             "`E3_MOVE_PAIRS` = `%s`.\n" % (E3_N, _repr(E3_MOVE_PAIRS)))
    stdout| +    L.append("- **E4:** canonical move form is `{e_in, e_out}`; `{d, s}` (degree & same-house) is "
    stdout| +             "accepted only as a **documented alternate** (`e_in = s`, `e_out = d - s`). If both forms "
    stdout| +             "appear in one move and disagree, the schema is contradictory and rejected. Max degree "
    stdout| +             "`E4_MAX_DEGREE` = `%d`; `E4_TWO_HOUSES` = `%d`.\n" % (E4_MAX_DEGREE, E4_TWO_HOUSES))
    stdout| +    L.append("- **E7:** explicit sequence, window width `E7_WINDOW` = `%d`, modulus `E7_MODULUS` = "
    stdout| +             "`%d`, and flip samples; a sample sequence must be long enough to realize its window "
    stdout| +             "width (a shorter sequence with a declared width is a structural contradiction). Odd-width "
    stdout| +             "control uses `E7_ODD_WINDOW` = `%d`.\n" % (E7_WINDOW, E7_MODULUS, E7_ODD_WINDOW))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    L.append("## Pinned source identities\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("| identity | value |")
    stdout| +    L.append("|---|---|")
    stdout| +    L.append("| contract refs | %s |" % _repr(PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS))
    stdout| +    L.append("| contract commits | %s |" % _repr(PINNED_CONTRACT_COMMITS))
    stdout| +    L.append("| contract content digests | %s |" % _repr(PINNED_CONTRACT_DIGESTS))
    stdout| +    L.append("| in-tree contract paths | %s |" % _repr(PINNED_CONTRACT_IN_TREE))
    stdout| +    L.append("| rubric ref | `%s` (commit `%s`, digest `%s`) |" % (
    stdout| +        PINNED_RUBRIC_REF, PINNED_RUBRIC_COMMIT, PINNED_RUBRIC_DIGEST))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    L.append("\n## Notes\n")
    stdout| +    L.append("- A role only selects a handler; it does not itself assert a fact.")
    stdout| +    L.append("- Unknown payload keys are tolerated (ignored by the extractor); this layer reports "
    stdout| +             "known-field validity rather than rejecting unknown keys, matching extractor semantics.")
    stdout| +    L.append("- This generated output is deterministic: it embeds no timestamps and no run-specific "
    stdout| +             "state. Execution time and test results live in the separate verification artifact.")
    stdout| +    L.append("")
    stdout| +    return "\n".join(L)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _contract_num(contract):
    stdout| +    return {"E3": 3, "E4": 4, "E7": 7}.get(contract)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _mdfield(name):
    stdout| +    return "`%s`" % name
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _repr(value):
    stdout| +    return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +if __name__ == "__main__":
    stdout| +    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))
