    stdout| +#!/usr/bin/env python3
    stdout| +"""cur_pipeline.py — one-command entrypoint: frozen bundle -> evidence -> verdict.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Runs the full grader lane on a single frozen G-ENG-style bundle:
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    bundle -> tools/cur_extract_evidence.py -> evidence manifest
    stdout| +           -> tools/cur_grade_artifact.py -> C1..C6 verdicts
    stdout| +
    stdout| +and emits a single combined transcript in which extractor diagnostics and grader
    stdout| +results are kept as separate sections. It is pure glue over the two existing tools —
    stdout| +no new policy, no re-implementation of the checked handlers or the grader ruleset.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +SCOPE / HOST TOOL BOUNDARY
    stdout| +--------------------------
    stdout| +Host-side tooling under tools/, exactly like the extractor and grader. It does not
    stdout| +construct machine values or machine terms and is not part of the machine runtime.
    stdout| +It deliberately avoids isinstance/type/getattr/callable.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +GATE RULES (hardening)
    stdout| +----------------------
    stdout| +* Continuation is permitted ONLY when the extractor reports a documented status:
    stdout| +  exit 0 (no proof debt) or exit 1 (proof debt). An unexpected exit, a signal, a
    stdout| +  timeout, a missing manifest, or an invalid manifest identity is an EXECUTION ERROR
    stdout| +  and is never graded.
    stdout| +* Extraction always lands in a fresh, invocation-owned temporary directory. A manifest
    stdout| +  left by an earlier run is never read as a grading input. The requested --out manifest
    stdout| +  is published atomically only after extraction + identity validation succeed.
    stdout| +* The manifest identity (bundle digest, extractor name + digest, schema version) is
    stdout| +  validated against this invocation's input bundle and tools before grading.
    stdout| +* Unknown CLI options and ambiguous bundle directories are rejected.
    stdout| +* Outputs are published atomically and the input bundle is never overwritten.
    stdout| +* Only invocation-owned temporary files are cleaned, including on failure paths.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +USAGE
    stdout| +  python3 tools/cur_pipeline.py <bundle.json|bundle_dir> \
    stdout| +         [--out <manifest.json>] [--transcript <file>] [--timeout <seconds>]
    stdout| +
    stdout| +ENV TEST SEAMS (host tooling only)
    stdout| +  CUR_PIPELINE_EXTRACTOR  overrides the extractor command path (a python module).
    stdout| +  CUR_PIPELINE_GRADER     overrides the grader command path (a python module).
    stdout| +
    stdout| +EXIT
    stdout| +  0  graded: final verdict PASS (all six checks PASS)
    stdout| +  1  graded: final verdict FAIL (one or more checks FAIL)
    stdout| +  2  either (a) a graded final verdict AMBIGUOUS / MALFORMED / schema / ruleset
    stdout| +     mismatch, or (b) not graded: extractor rejected the bundle (exit 2), or
    stdout| +     (c) not graded: EXECUTION ERROR (unexpected extractor/grader exit, signal,
    stdout| +     timeout, invalid manifest, or output-write failure)
    stdout| +  3  graded: no FAIL but at least one CANNOT_DETERMINE
    stdout| +
    stdout| +The transcript always carries a PIPELINE_STATUS line that distinguishes an EXECUTION
    stdout| +ERROR from a graded verdict, so a caller can tell "the tooling failed" from "the
    stdout| +artifact failed." The numeric 0/1/2/3 interface is preserved; execution errors are
    stdout| +reported separately and never converted into a PASS.
    stdout| +"""
    stdout| +
    stdout| +import json
    stdout| +import os
    stdout| +import subprocess
    stdout| +import sys
    stdout| +import tempfile
    stdout| +
    stdout| +HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    stdout| +EXTRACTOR = os.environ.get("CUR_PIPELINE_EXTRACTOR") or os.path.join(HERE, "cur_extract_evidence.py")
    stdout| +GRADER = os.environ.get("CUR_PIPELINE_GRADER") or os.path.join(HERE, "cur_grade_artifact.py")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# Documented extractor statuses that permit continuation (grade).
    stdout| +_EXTRACTOR_OK = frozenset((0, 1))
    stdout| +# Documented grader verdicts, one per line of the exit contract above.
    stdout| +_GRADER_OK = frozenset((0, 1, 2, 3))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +_USAGE = (
    stdout| +    "usage: cur_pipeline.py <bundle.json|bundle_dir> "
    stdout| +    "[--out <manifest.json>] [--transcript <file>] [--timeout <seconds>]"
    stdout| +)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _sha256_text(text):
    stdout| +    import hashlib
    stdout| +    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _read_text(path):
    stdout| +    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +        return f.read()
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _bundle_digest_from_file(path):
    stdout| +    """Reproduce the extractor's bundle digest: lexicographic JSON, compact separators."""
    stdout| +    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +        bundle = json.load(f)
    stdout| +    cannon = json.dumps(bundle, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    stdout| +    return _sha256_text(cannon)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _extractor_digest():
    stdout| +    """sha256 of the extractor source as observed by a host consumer."""
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        return _sha256_text(_read_text(EXTRACTOR))
    stdout| +    except OSError:
    stdout| +        return "unavailable"
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _graded_verdict(stdout):
    stdout| +    """Return the grader's verdict string if stdout carries a verdict object, else None."""
    stdout| +    text = (stdout or "").strip()
    stdout| +    if not text:
    stdout| +        return None
    stdout| +    tail = text.rsplit("\n", 1)[-1].strip()
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        obj = json.loads(tail)
    stdout| +    except ValueError:
    stdout| +        return None
    stdout| +    final = obj.get("final")
    stdout| +    if not isinstance(final, str):
    stdout| +        return None
    stdout| +    return final
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _atomic_write_text(path, text):
    stdout| +    """Write text to a same-directory temp then atomically replace path. Never touches input."""
    stdout| +    d = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path)) or "."
    stdout| +    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".cur_pipeline_pub_", dir=d)
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +            f.write(text)
    stdout| +        os.replace(tmp, path)
    stdout| +    except OSError:
    stdout| +        try:
    stdout| +            os.unlink(tmp)
    stdout| +        except OSError:
    stdout| +            pass
    stdout| +        raise
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _atomic_copy(src, dst):
    stdout| +    """Copy src to dst atomically (content inspectable only after a clean replace)."""
    stdout| +    text = _read_text(src)
    stdout| +    _atomic_write_text(dst, text)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _emit(text):
    stdout| +    print(text)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def main(argv):
    stdout| +    if len(argv) < 2:
    stdout| +        sys.stderr.write(_USAGE + "\n")
    stdout| +        return 2
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    src = argv[1]
    stdout| +    out_manifest = None
    stdout| +    transcript_path = None
    stdout| +    timeout = None
    stdout| +    i = 2
    stdout| +    while i < len(argv):
    stdout| +        arg = argv[i]
    stdout| +        if arg == "--out" and i + 1 < len(argv):
    stdout| +            out_manifest = argv[i + 1]
    stdout| +            i += 2
    stdout| +        elif arg == "--transcript" and i + 1 < len(argv):
    stdout| +            transcript_path = argv[i + 1]
    stdout| +            i += 2
    stdout| +        elif arg == "--timeout" and i + 1 < len(argv):
    stdout| +            try:
    stdout| +                timeout = float(argv[i + 1])
    stdout| +            except ValueError:
    stdout| +                sys.stderr.write("pipeline: --timeout must be a number\n")
    stdout| +                return 2
    stdout| +            if timeout <= 0:
    stdout| +                sys.stderr.write("pipeline: --timeout must be positive\n")
    stdout| +                return 2
    stdout| +            i += 2
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            sys.stderr.write(_USAGE + "\n")
    stdout| +            sys.stderr.write("pipeline: unknown argument: %s\n" % (arg,))
    stdout| +            return 2
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # Resolve a bundle directory to a single json file; reject ambiguous directories.
    stdout| +    if os.path.isdir(src):
    stdout| +        jsons = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(src) if f.endswith(".json"))
    stdout| +        if len(jsons) != 1:
    stdout| +            sys.stderr.write("pipeline: directory must contain exactly one bundle json\n")
    stdout| +            return 2
    stdout| +        src = os.path.join(src, jsons[0])
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # Never overwrite the input bundle with an output.
    stdout| +    abs_src = os.path.abspath(src)
    stdout| +    for label, target in (("--out", out_manifest), ("--transcript", transcript_path)):
    stdout| +        if target is not None and os.path.abspath(target) == abs_src:
    stdout| +            sys.stderr.write("pipeline: refusing to overwrite the input bundle via %s\n" % (label,))
    stdout| +            return 2
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- phase 1: extract into an invocation-owned temp manifest (never a stale --out).
    stdout| +    tmp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="cur_pipeline_")
    stdout| +    extract_manifest = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "manifest.json")
    stdout| +    transcript_lines = [
    stdout| +        "CUR-GRADER-ENG pipeline: %s" % os.path.basename(src),
    stdout| +        "  extractor: %s" % EXTRACTOR,
    stdout| +        "  grader:    %s" % GRADER,
    stdout| +        "=" * 70,
    stdout| +    ]
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    def _finish(exit_code, status, extra_lines):
    stdout| +        transcript_lines.append("=" * 70)
    stdout| +        transcript_lines.append("PIPELINE_STATUS=%s" % status)
    stdout| +        transcript_lines.append("PIPELINE_EXIT=%s" % exit_code)
    stdout| +        out_text = "\n".join(transcript_lines) + "\n"
    stdout| +        try:
    stdout| +            if transcript_path is not None:
    stdout| +                _atomic_write_text(transcript_path, out_text)
    stdout| +            else:
    stdout| +                _emit(out_text.rstrip("\n"))
    stdout| +        except OSError as e:
    stdout| +            sys.stderr.write("pipeline: cannot write transcript: %s\n" % (e,))
    stdout| +            return 2
    stdout| +        return exit_code
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        # Run the extractor; capture diagnostics separately.
    stdout| +        try:
    stdout| +            ex = subprocess.run([sys.executable, EXTRACTOR, src, "--out", extract_manifest],
    stdout| +                                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
    stdout| +        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[extractor] TIMEOUT")
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[pipeline] extractor exceeded timeout; not grading")
    stdout| +            return _finish(2, "EXECUTION_ERROR", [])
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        extractor_exit = ex.returncode
    stdout| +        extractor_stdout = ex.stdout.strip()
    stdout| +        extractor_stderr = ex.stderr.strip()
    stdout| +        transcript_lines.append("[extractor] exit=%s" % extractor_exit)
    stdout| +        if extractor_stdout:
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[extractor] " + extractor_stdout)
    stdout| +        if extractor_stderr:
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[extractor] stderr: " + extractor_stderr)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # Gate on documented statuses only: 0 or 1. Anything else (incl. signals, exit 7)
    stdout| +        # is an execution error, not a grade and not a reject.
    stdout| +        if extractor_exit not in _EXTRACTOR_OK:
    stdout| +            if extractor_exit == 2:
    stdout| +                # Documented extractor rejection: meaningful downstream, never graded.
    stdout| +                transcript_lines.append("[pipeline] extractor rejected bundle; not grading (exit 2)")
    stdout| +                return _finish(2, "REJECTED", [])
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append(
    stdout| +                "[pipeline] unexpected extractor exit %s; not grading" % (extractor_exit,))
    stdout| +            return _finish(2, "EXECUTION_ERROR", [])
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        if not os.path.exists(extract_manifest):
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[pipeline] extractor produced no manifest; not grading")
    stdout| +            return _finish(2, "EXECUTION_ERROR", [])
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- validate manifest identity against this invocation's input and tools.
    stdout| +        try:
    stdout| +            manifest = json.loads(_read_text(extract_manifest))
    stdout| +            identity = manifest.get("identity")
    stdout| +            if identity is None:
    stdout| +                raise ValueError("manifest has no identity block")
    stdout| +            fmt_digest = _bundle_digest_from_file(src)
    stdout| +            if identity.get("bundle_digest") != fmt_digest:
    stdout| +                raise ValueError("manifest bundle digest does not match input bundle")
    stdout| +            if identity.get("extractor") != "cur_extract_evidence.py" and \
    stdout| +               identity.get("extractor") != os.path.basename(EXTRACTOR):
    stdout| +                raise ValueError("manifest extractor does not match this extractor")
    stdout| +            if identity.get("extractor_digest") != _extractor_digest():
    stdout| +                raise ValueError("manifest extractor digest does not match this extractor")
    stdout| +            if identity.get("grader_schema_version") is None or \
    stdout| +               manifest.get("schema_version") is None:
    stdout| +                raise ValueError("manifest missing schema version")
    stdout| +        except (ValueError, OSError) as e:
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[pipeline] manifest identity invalid: %s" % (e,))
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[pipeline] not grading")
    stdout| +            return _finish(2, "EXECUTION_ERROR", [])
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # Phase 1 succeeded: atomically publish the validated manifest to --out if requested.
    stdout| +        if out_manifest is not None:
    stdout| +            try:
    stdout| +                _atomic_copy(extract_manifest, out_manifest)
    stdout| +            except OSError:
    stdout| +                transcript_lines.append("[pipeline] cannot publish manifest to --out; not grading")
    stdout| +                return _finish(2, "EXECUTION_ERROR", [])
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- phase 2: grade.
    stdout| +        try:
    stdout| +            gr = subprocess.run([sys.executable, GRADER, extract_manifest],
    stdout| +                                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
    stdout| +        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[grader] TIMEOUT")
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[pipeline] grader exceeded timeout; no verdict")
    stdout| +            return _finish(2, "EXECUTION_ERROR", [])
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        grader_exit = gr.returncode
    stdout| +        grader_stdout = gr.stdout.strip()
    stdout| +        grader_stderr = gr.stderr.strip()
    stdout| +        transcript_lines.append("[grader] exit=%s" % grader_exit)
    stdout| +        if grader_stdout:
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[grader] " + grader_stdout)
    stdout| +        if grader_stderr:
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append("[grader] stderr: " + grader_stderr)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # A grader verdict requires BOTH a documented exit AND a parseable verdict object.
    stdout| +        verdict = _graded_verdict(grader_stdout)
    stdout| +        if grader_exit not in _GRADER_OK or verdict is None:
    stdout| +            transcript_lines.append(
    stdout| +                "[pipeline] grader did not produce a verdict (exit %s); no verdict" % (grader_exit,))
    stdout| +            return _finish(2, "EXECUTION_ERROR", [])
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        return _finish(grader_exit, "GRADED", [])
    stdout| +    finally:
    stdout| +        import shutil
    stdout| +        shutil.rmtree(tmp_dir, ignore_errors=True)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +if __name__ == "__main__":
    stdout| +    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))
    stdout| diff --git a/tools/tests/cur_extractor/fixtures/e3-circular-support.json b/tools/tests/cur_extractor/fixtures/e3-circular-support.json
    stdout| new file mode 100644

    stdout| --- /dev/null

    stdout| diff --git a/tools/tests/cur_extractor/run.py b/tools/tests/cur_extractor/run.py
    stdout| new file mode 100644
    stdout| index 0000000..53e9a7b
    stdout| --- /dev/null
    stdout| +++ b/tools/tests/cur_extractor/run.py
    stdout| @@ -0,0 +1,420 @@
    stdout| +#!/usr/bin/env python3
    stdout| +"""run.py — selftest for tools/cur_extract_evidence.py (checked evidence handlers).
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Runs the END-TO-END path for every frozen-style fixture:
    stdout| +    frozen G-ENG-style bundle -> cur_extract_evidence.py -> evidence manifest
    stdout| +    -> cur_grade_artifact.py -> C1..C6 verdicts
    stdout| +
    stdout| +It asserts the extractor's EVIDENCE DISCIPLINE: a recognized role alone must NOT establish PASS;
    stdout| +the checked handlers must derive verdicts from cited numeric payloads; the proof-support dependency
    stdout| +chain must reject self-citation / circular / unresolved support; malformed bundles must yield
    stdout| +structured exit-2 errors; the manifest must bind to immutable identities; and every run must use a
    stdout| +unique scratch directory so concurrent invocations cannot collide.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +This harness reads only the fixtures it owns and the manifests it produces. It never reads the
    stdout| +grader's sealed expected-results table (oracle.json is intentionally absent in the production-path
    stdout| +probe). Exit 0 if all pass; non-zero otherwise.
    stdout| +"""
    stdout| +
    stdout| +import concurrent.futures
    stdout| +import json
    stdout| +import os
    stdout| +import shutil
    stdout| +import subprocess
    stdout| +import sys
    stdout| +import tempfile
    stdout| +import time
    stdout| +
    stdout| +HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    stdout| +REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE)))
    stdout| +EXTRACTOR = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_extract_evidence.py")
    stdout| +GRADER = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_grade_artifact.py")
    stdout| +FIXDIR = os.path.join(HERE, "fixtures")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def extract(bundle_path, out_path, cwd=REPO):
    stdout| +    p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, EXTRACTOR, bundle_path, "--out", out_path],
    stdout| +                       cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    stdout| +    return p.returncode, p.stdout.strip(), p.stderr.strip()
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def run_grader(manifest_path, cwd=REPO):
    stdout| +    p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, GRADER, manifest_path], cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        parsed = json.loads(p.stdout.strip())
    stdout| +    except ValueError:
    stdout| +        parsed = {"error": p.stdout.strip(), "stderr": p.stderr.strip()}
    stdout| +    return p.returncode, parsed
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def extract_and_grade(bundle_path, scratch_dir):
    stdout| +    """Full pipeline for one bundle. Returns (extractor_rc, grader_rc, grader_parsed, manifest)."""
    stdout| +    key = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(bundle_path))[0]
    stdout| +    mpath = os.path.join(scratch_dir, key + ".manifest.json")
    stdout| +    xrc, _, _ = extract(bundle_path, mpath)
    stdout| +    if not os.path.exists(mpath):
    stdout| +        return xrc, None, None, None
    stdout| +    with open(mpath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +        manifest = json.load(f)
    stdout| +    grc, gparsed = run_grader(mpath)
    stdout| +    return xrc, grc, gparsed, manifest
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# --------------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
    stdout| +def _final(d):
    stdout| +    return d.get("final") if d else None
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _disc(d):
    stdout| +    return d.get("discrepancy") if d else None
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _evidence_of(manifest, check):
    stdout| +    return manifest["evidence"].get(check, {})
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _has_pass(manifest, check, key):
    stdout| +    return bool(_evidence_of(manifest, check).get(key))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _has_fail(manifest, check, key):
    stdout| +    return bool(_evidence_of(manifest, check).get(key))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _probe_noiseless(scratch):
    stdout| +    """Run the production pipeline with the grader's sealed expected-results table unavailable."""
    stdout| +    # Copy the two tools + one fixture into an isolated dir with NO oracle.json present anywhere.
    stdout| +    iso = os.path.join(scratch, "probe_no_oracle")
    stdout| +    os.makedirs(iso, exist_ok=True)
    stdout| +    shutil.copy(EXTRACTOR, iso)
    stdout| +    shutil.copy(GRADER, iso)
    stdout| +    shutil.copy(os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-pass.json"), iso)
    stdout| +    cmd1 = [sys.executable, "cur_extract_evidence.py", "e3-pass.json", "--out", "m.json"]
    stdout| +    p1 = subprocess.run(cmd1, cwd=iso, capture_output=True, text=True)
    stdout| +    cmd2 = [sys.executable, "cur_grade_artifact.py", "m.json"]
    stdout| +    p2 = subprocess.run(cmd2, cwd=iso, capture_output=True, text=True)
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        parsed = json.loads(p2.stdout.strip())
    stdout| +    except ValueError:
    stdout| +        parsed = {"error": p2.stdout.strip()}
    stdout| +    ok = (p1.returncode == 0) and (p2.returncode == 0) and (parsed.get("final") == "PASS")
    stdout| +    # ensure oracle.json genuinely absent in the probe tree
    stdout| +    oracle_absent = not os.path.exists(os.path.join(iso, "oracle.json")) \
    stdout| +        and not os.path.exists(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(iso), "oracle.json"))
    stdout| +    return ok, oracle_absent
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# --------------------------------------------------------------------- main
    stdout| +def main(argv):
    stdout| +    child = "--child" in argv
    stdout| +    scratch = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="cur_extractor_selftest_")
    stdout| +    failures = []
    stdout| +    summary = {"checks": 0, "ok": 0}
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    def record(label, ok, detail=""):
    stdout| +        summary["checks"] += 1
    stdout| +        if ok:
    stdout| +            summary["ok"] += 1
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            failures.append((label, detail))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +    print("EXTRACTOR SELFTEST (checked evidence) — tools/tests/cur_extractor/run.py")
    stdout| +    print("scratch=%s" % scratch)
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- CHECK 1: e3-pass is the ONE full END-TO-END PASS path.
    stdout| +    print("CHECK 1 — e3-pass end-to-end yields full PASS (all six checks PASS)")
    stdout| +    xrc, grc, gparsed, manifest = extract_and_grade(os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-pass.json"), scratch)
    stdout| +    ok1 = (xrc == 0) and (grc == 0) and (_final(gparsed) == "PASS")
    stdout| +    record("check1 e3-pass full PASS", ok1, "xrc=%s grc=%s final=%s" % (xrc, grc, _final(gparsed)))
    stdout| +    print("  xrc=%s grc=%s final=%s (%s)" % (xrc, grc, _final(gparsed), "OK" if ok1 else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +    print("")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- CHECK 2: role-only negative control must NOT be a PASS.
    stdout| +    print("CHECK 2 — role-only negative control is NOT a silent PASS")
    stdout| +    xrc, grc, gparsed, manifest = extract_and_grade(os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-role-only-negative-control.json"), scratch)
    stdout| +    not_pass = _final(gparsed) != "PASS"
    stdout| +    ok2 = not_pass
    stdout| +    record("check2 role-only not PASS", ok2, "final=%s" % (_final(gparsed)))
    stdout| +    print("  final=%s evidence=%s (%s)" % (_final(gparsed), {k: v for k, v in manifest["evidence"].items() if v}, "OK" if ok2 else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +    print("")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- CHECK 3: recognized role + empty support must NOT be a PASS.
    stdout| +    print("CHECK 3 — recognized role + empty support is NOT a PASS (all CD)")
    stdout| +    xrc, grc, gparsed, manifest = extract_and_grade(os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-empty-support.json"), scratch)
    stdout| +    all_cd = (_final(gparsed) == "CANNOT_DETERMINE") and all(not v for v in manifest["evidence"].values())
    stdout| +    ok3 = all_cd
    stdout| +    record("check3 empty support not PASS", ok3, "final=%s" % (_final(gparsed)))
    stdout| +    print("  final=%s evidence=%s (%s)" % (_final(gparsed), {k: v for k, v in manifest["evidence"].items() if v}, "OK" if ok3 else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +    print("")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- CHECK 4: recognized role + unrelated support (unknown cite) must NOT be a PASS.
    stdout| +    print("CHECK 4 — recognized role + unrelated/unresolved support is NOT a PASS")
    stdout| +    xrc, grc, gparsed, manifest = extract_and_grade(os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-unrelated-support.json"), scratch)
    stdout| +    ok4 = (_final(gparsed) != "PASS") and (xrc == 1)
    stdout| +    record("check4 unrelated support not PASS", ok4, "xrc=%s final=%s" % (xrc, _final(gparsed)))
    stdout| +    print("  xrc=%s final=%s (%s)" % (xrc, _final(gparsed), "OK" if ok4 else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +    print("")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- CHECK 5: self-citation cannot justify its own claim.
    stdout| +    print("CHECK 5 — self-citation cannot justify a claim (all CD + proof diagnostic)")
    stdout| +    xrc, grc, gparsed, manifest = extract_and_grade(os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-self-citation.json"), scratch)
    stdout| +    diags = manifest.get("extractor_diagnostics", [])
    stdout| +    has_self = any("self-citation" in d for d in diags)
    stdout| +    ok5 = (xrc == 1) and (_final(gparsed) != "PASS") and has_self
    stdout| +    record("check5 self-citation rejected", ok5, "xrc=%s final=%s self_diag=%s" % (xrc, _final(gparsed), has_self))
    stdout| +    print("  xrc=%s final=%s self_diag=%s (%s)" % (xrc, _final(gparsed), has_self, "OK" if ok5 else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +    print("")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- CHECK 6: circular proof support cannot justify a claim.
    stdout| +    print("CHECK 6 — circular support cannot justify a claim (all CD + proof diagnostic)")
    stdout| +    xrc, grc, gparsed, manifest = extract_and_grade(os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-circular-support.json"), scratch)
    stdout| +    diags = manifest.get("extractor_diagnostics", [])
    stdout| +    has_cycle = any("circular support" in d for d in diags)
    stdout| +    ok6 = (xrc == 1) and (_final(gparsed) != "PASS") and has_cycle
    stdout| +    record("check6 circular support rejected", ok6, "xrc=%s final=%s cycle_diag=%s" % (xrc, _final(gparsed), has_cycle))
    stdout| +    print("  xrc=%s final=%s cycle_diag=%s (%s)" % (xrc, _final(gparsed), has_cycle, "OK" if ok6 else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +    print("")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- CHECK 7: unresolved upstream support cannot justify dependent evidence.
    stdout| +    print("CHECK 7 — unresolved upstream support blocks dependent evidence")

    stdout| +    conc = ["e3-pass.json", "e3-partial-missing-odd-gen.json"]
    stdout| +    def one(fname):
    stdout| +        return extract_and_grade(os.path.join(FIXDIR, fname), scratch)
    stdout| +    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as ex:
    stdout| +        futures = [ex.submit(one, fn) for fn in conc]
    stdout| +        results = [fut.result() for fut in futures]
    stdout| +    isolated = True
    stdout| +    # compare against serial runs into distinct scratch subdirs
    stdout| +    for i, fn in enumerate(conc):
    stdout| +        sub = os.path.join(scratch, "serial_%d" % i)

    stdout| +        if results[i][3]["evidence"] != serial[3]["evidence"] or \
    stdout| +           results[i][3]["citations"] != serial[3]["citations"]:
    stdout| +            isolated = False

    stdout| +        print("CHECK 18 — two complete selftests run concurrently (unique scratch each)")
    stdout| +        env = dict(os.environ)
    stdout| +        # Mark grandchildren as no-concurrency so they terminate.
    stdout| +        env["CUR_EXTRACTOR_NO_CONC"] = "1"
    stdout| +        con = []
    stdout| +        for _ in range(2):
    stdout| +            con.append(subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, os.path.abspath(__file__), "--child"],
    stdout| +                                        cwd=REPO, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
    stdout| +                                        env=env))
    stdout| +        codes = [p.wait() for p in con]
    stdout| +        ok18 = all(c == 0 for c in codes)
    stdout| +        record("check18 two concurrent selftests", ok18, "codes=%s" % codes)
    stdout| +        print("  exit codes=%s (%s)" % (codes, "OK" if ok18 else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +        print("")
    stdout| +    elif os.environ.get("CUR_EXTRACTOR_NO_CONC") != "1":
    stdout| +        # A --child used as part of CHECK 18; it should never recurse (it already ran 1..17 above).
    stdout| +        pass

    stdout| diff --git a/tools/tests/cur_grader/run.py b/tools/tests/cur_grader/run.py
    stdout| new file mode 100644
    stdout| index 0000000..9b999b8
    stdout| --- /dev/null
    stdout| +++ b/tools/tests/cur_grader/run.py
    stdout| @@ -0,0 +1,236 @@
    stdout| +#!/usr/bin/env python3
    stdout| +"""run.py — selftest for tools/cur_grade_artifact.py.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Runs every fixture through the grader as a subprocess and asserts the emitted
    stdout| +result, discrepancy label and exit status match the SEPARATE oracle (oracle.json).
    stdout| +The oracle is read ONLY here; the grader never reads it.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Hardening (post-571c9d1) coverage:
    stdout| +  * every fixture -> expected final/discrepancy/exit (subprocess);
    stdout| +  * changing a fixture's decorative expected-label field does NOT change the result;
    stdout| +  * removing required evidence changes PASS -> CANNOT_DETERMINE;
    stdout| +  * contradictory evidence exits 2;
    stdout| +  * each failing fixture emits EXACTLY one discrepancy label;
    stdout| +  * multi-taxonomy ambiguity (E4 C1+C3, E7 C2+C3) exits 2;
    stdout| +  * string "false", numeric 1, array, object evidence -> exit 2;
    stdout| +  * unknown evidence key -> exit 2 (must not silently become CANNOT_DETERMINE);
    stdout| +  * ruleset_id mismatch -> exit 2;
    stdout| +  * missing evidence (CD) remains CANNOT_DETERMINE, exit 3;
    stdout| +  * two grader processes concurrently -> identical COMPLETE JSON (exit+final+discrepancy+checks);
    stdout| +  * running from a cwd outside the repository.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Exit 0 if all pass; non-zero (count of failures) otherwise.
    stdout| +"""
    stdout| +
    stdout| +import json
    stdout| +import os
    stdout| +import subprocess
    stdout| +import sys
    stdout| +import concurrent.futures
    stdout| +
    stdout| +HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    stdout| +REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE)))
    stdout| +GRADER = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_grade_artifact.py")
    stdout| +FIXDIR = os.path.join(HERE, "fixtures")
    stdout| +ORACLE = os.path.join(HERE, "oracle.json")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def load_oracle():
    stdout| +    with open(ORACLE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +        return json.load(f)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def run_grader(path, cwd=None):
    stdout| +    """Run the grader subprocess on a fixture; return (exit, parsed_json, raw)."""
    stdout| +    cmd = [sys.executable, GRADER, path]
    stdout| +    p = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    stdout| +    raw = p.stdout.strip()
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        parsed = json.loads(raw)
    stdout| +    except ValueError:
    stdout| +        parsed = {"error": raw, "stderr": p.stderr.strip()}
    stdout| +    return p.returncode, parsed, raw
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def main():
    stdout| +    oracle = load_oracle()
    stdout| +    failures = []
    stdout| +    summary = {"checks": 0, "ok": 0, "fail": 0}
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    def record(label, ok, detail=""):
    stdout| +        summary["checks"] += 1
    stdout| +        if ok:
    stdout| +            summary["ok"] += 1
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            summary["fail"] += 1
    stdout| +            failures.append((label, detail))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    fixtures = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(FIXDIR) if f.endswith(".json"))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +    print("GRADER SELFTEST (hardened) — tools/tests/cur_grader/run.py")
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- CHECK 1: run every fixture, compare to oracle.
    stdout| +    print(f"{'fixture':<20}{'exit':<6}{'final':<24}{'discrepancy':<24}{'ok'}")
    stdout| +    print("-" * 78)
    stdout| +    for f in fixtures:
    stdout| +        path = os.path.join(FIXDIR, f)
    stdout| +        fid = f[:-5]
    stdout| +        exp = oracle.get(fid)
    stdout| +        if exp is None:
    stdout| +            record("oracle-missing " + fid, False, "no oracle entry")
    stdout| +            print(f"{fid:<20}{'?':<6}{'-':<24}{'-':<24}{'NO-ORACLE'}")
    stdout| +            continue
    stdout| +        rc, parsed, _ = run_grader(path, cwd=REPO)
    stdout| +        final = parsed.get("final", "?")
    stdout| +        disc = parsed.get("discrepancy", "?")
    stdout| +        ok = (rc == exp["exit"]) and (final == exp["final"]) and (disc == exp["discrepancy"])
    stdout| +        record("check1 " + fid, ok,
    stdout| +               "got exit=%s final=%s disc=%s want exit=%s final=%s disc=%s" % (
    stdout| +                   rc, final, disc, exp["exit"], exp["final"], exp["discrepancy"]))
    stdout| +        print(f"{fid:<20}{str(rc):<6}{final:<24}{disc:<24}{'Y' if ok else 'N'}")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    print("-" * 78)
    stdout| +    check1_fail = sum(1 for lbl, _ in failures if lbl.startswith("check1 "))

    stdout| +#!/usr/bin/env python3
    stdout| +"""hardening.py — regression tests for tools/cur_pipeline.py gate hardening.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Exercises the execution-error / publication / concurrency behaviors that the baseline
    stdout| +selftest (run.py) does not cover:
    stdout| +
    stdout| +  * successful fixture -> 0 GRADED
    stdout| +  * valid FAIL fixture  -> 1 GRADED
    stdout| +  * valid CANNOT_DETERMINE fixture -> 3 GRADED
    stdout| +  * extractor prints plausible output then exits 7 -> 2 EXECUTION_ERROR (never graded)
    stdout| +  * stale --out manifest present -> replaced only with a validated fresh manifest
    stdout| +  * extractor times out -> 2 EXECUTION_ERROR
    stdout| +  * extractor killed by signal -> 2 EXECUTION_ERROR
    stdout| +  * grader exits without a verdict -> 2 EXECUTION_ERROR
    stdout| +  * unwritable transcript -> 2 (output-write failure, no partial verdict)
    stdout| +  * malformed input -> 2 REJECTED (never graded)
    stdout| +  * foreign working directory
    stdout| +  * two complete pipeline invocations concurrently
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Exit 0 if all pass; 1 otherwise. Uses only subprocess + tempfile; never edits the
    stdout| +extractor or grader source and never mutates the fixture tree.
    stdout| +"""
    stdout| +
    stdout| +import json
    stdout| +import os
    stdout| +import shutil
    stdout| +import subprocess
    stdout| +import sys
    stdout| +import tempfile
    stdout| +
    stdout| +HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    stdout| +REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE)))
    stdout| +PIPELINE = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_pipeline.py")
    stdout| +FIXDIR = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "tests", "cur_extractor", "fixtures")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _sha(text):
    stdout| +    import hashlib
    stdout| +    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _run(scratch, bundle, *extra, env=None, cwd=None, timeout=30):
    stdout| +    out = os.path.join(scratch, "out.json")
    stdout| +    trans = os.path.join(scratch, "t.txt")
    stdout| +    cmd = [sys.executable, PIPELINE, bundle, "--out", out, "--transcript", trans] + list(extra)
    stdout| +    merged = dict(os.environ)
    stdout| +    if env:
    stdout| +        merged.update(env)
    stdout| +    p = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=cwd, env=merged)
    stdout| +    transcript = ""
    stdout| +    if os.path.exists(trans):
    stdout| +        with open(trans, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +            transcript = f.read()
    stdout| +    return p.returncode, transcript, out, p.stderr
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _bundle_digest(path):
    stdout| +    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +        b = json.load(f)
    stdout| +    return _sha(json.dumps(b, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _write(path, text):
    stdout| +    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +        f.write(text)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def main():
    stdout| +    scratch = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="cur_pipeline_hardening_")
    stdout| +    failures = []
    stdout| +    total = 0
    stdout| +    ok = 0
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    def check(label, cond, detail=""):
    stdout| +        nonlocal total, ok
    stdout| +        total += 1
    stdout| +        if cond:
    stdout| +            ok += 1
    stdout| +            print("  %-46s OK" % (label,))
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            failures.append((label, detail))
    stdout| +            print("  %-46s FAIL  %s" % (label, detail))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +        print("PIPELINE HARDENING — tools/tests/cur_pipeline/hardening.py")
    stdout| +        print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +        fx = os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-pass.json")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 1. successful fixture.
    stdout| +        rc, trans, out, _ = _run(scratch, fx)
    stdout| +        check("successful fixture -> 0", rc == 0 and "PIPELINE_STATUS=GRADED" in trans,
    stdout| +              "rc=%s" % rc)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 2. valid FAIL.
    stdout| +        failb = os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e4-degree4-move.json")
    stdout| +        rc, trans, _, _ = _run(scratch, failb)
    stdout| +        check("valid FAIL -> 1", rc == 1 and "PIPELINE_STATUS=GRADED" in trans,
    stdout| +              "rc=%s" % rc)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 3. valid CANNOT_DETERMINE.
    stdout| +        cdb = os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-role-only-negative-control.json")
    stdout| +        rc, trans, _, _ = _run(scratch, cdb)
    stdout| +        check("valid CANNOT_DETERMINE -> 3", rc == 3 and "PIPELINE_STATUS=GRADED" in trans,
    stdout| +              "rc=%s" % rc)
    stdout| +

    stdout| +            "json.dump({'schema_version': 1, 'evidence': {}, 'identity': {}}, sys.stdout)\n"
    stdout| +            "sys.exit(7)\n"
    stdout| +        ))
    stdout| +        rc, trans, _, _ = _run(scratch, fx, env={"CUR_PIPELINE_EXTRACTOR": eb})
    stdout| +        check("extractor exits 7 -> 2 EXECUTION_ERROR",
    stdout| +              rc == 2 and "PIPELINE_STATUS=EXECUTION_ERROR" in trans
    stdout| +              and "[grader]" not in trans and "[pipeline] unexpected extractor exit 7" in trans,
    stdout| +              "rc=%s" % rc)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 5. stale --out manifest present -> replaced with a validated fresh manifest.
    stdout| +        stale = os.path.join(scratch, "stale_out.json")
    stdout| +        _write(stale, json.dumps({"stale": True, "identity": {"bundle_digest": "deadbeef"}}))
    stdout| +        cmd = [sys.executable, PIPELINE, fx, "--out", stale,
    stdout| +               "--transcript", os.path.join(scratch, "stale_t.txt")]
    stdout| +        sp = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    stdout| +        matched = False
    stdout| +        if os.path.exists(stale):
    stdout| +            with open(stale, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +                try:
    stdout| +                    m = json.load(f)
    stdout| +                    matched = (m.get("identity", {}).get("bundle_digest") == _bundle_digest(fx))
    stdout| +                except ValueError:
    stdout| +                    matched = False
    stdout| +        check("stale --out replaced by validated manifest",
    stdout| +              sp.returncode == 0 and matched, "rc=%s matched=%s" % (sp.returncode, matched))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 6. extractor times out.
    stdout| +        tb = os.path.join(scratch, "hang_extractor.py")
    stdout| +        _write(tb, "import time\ntime.sleep(30)\n")
    stdout| +        rc, trans, _, _ = _run(scratch, fx, "--timeout", "1",
    stdout| +                               env={"CUR_PIPELINE_EXTRACTOR": tb}, timeout=15)
    stdout| +        check("extractor timeout -> 2 EXECUTION_ERROR",
    stdout| +              rc == 2 and "PIPELINE_STATUS=EXECUTION_ERROR" in trans
    stdout| +              and "[grader]" not in trans, "rc=%s" % rc)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 7. extractor killed by signal (negative returncode).
    stdout| +        kb = os.path.join(scratch, "kill_extractor.py")
    stdout| +        _write(kb, "import os, signal\nos.kill(os.getpid(), signal.SIGKILL)\n")
    stdout| +        rc, trans, _, _ = _run(scratch, fx, env={"CUR_PIPELINE_EXTRACTOR": kb})
    stdout| +        check("extractor killed -> 2 EXECUTION_ERROR",
    stdout| +              rc == 2 and "PIPELINE_STATUS=EXECUTION_ERROR" in trans,

    stdout| +        check("grader no verdict -> 2 EXECUTION_ERROR",
    stdout| +              rc == 2 and "PIPELINE_STATUS=EXECUTION_ERROR" in trans,
    stdout| +              "rc=%s" % rc)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 9. unwritable transcript (parent path is a file).
    stdout| +        blocker = os.path.join(scratch, "blockfile")
    stdout| +        _write(blocker, "not a directory\n")
    stdout| +        bad_trans = os.path.join(blocker, "x.txt")
    stdout| +        cmd = [sys.executable, PIPELINE, fx, "--out", os.path.join(scratch, "uo_out.json"),
    stdout| +               "--transcript", bad_trans]
    stdout| +        up = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    stdout| +        check("unwritable transcript -> 2 (no partial verdict)",
    stdout| +              up.returncode == 2 and "cannot write transcript" in up.stderr, "rc=%s" % up.returncode)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 10. malformed input -> 2 REJECTED / never graded.
    stdout| +        mb = os.path.join(FIXDIR, "malformed-duplicate-id.json")
    stdout| +        rc, trans, _, _ = _run(scratch, mb)
    stdout| +        check("malformed input -> 2 REJECTED",
    stdout| +              rc == 2 and "PIPELINE_STATUS=REJECTED" in trans and "[grader]" not in trans,
    stdout| +              "rc=%s status=%s" % (rc, "PIPELINE_STATUS=" + ("REJECTED" if "REJECTED" in trans else "?")))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 11. foreign working directory.
    stdout| +        extcwd = os.path.join(scratch, "foreign")
    stdout| +        os.makedirs(extcwd, exist_ok=True)
    stdout| +        rc, trans, out, _ = _run(scratch, fx, cwd=extcwd)
    stdout| +        check("foreign working directory", rc == 0 and os.path.exists(out), "rc=%s" % rc)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # 12. two complete pipeline invocations concurrently.
    stdout| +        b1 = os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-pass.json")
    stdout| +        b2 = os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e4-pass.json")
    stdout| +        o1 = os.path.join(scratch, "c1_out.json")

    stdout| +        t1 = os.path.join(scratch, "c1_t.txt")
    stdout| +        t2 = os.path.join(scratch, "c2_t.txt")
    stdout| +        p1 = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, PIPELINE, b1, "--out", o1, "--transcript", t1],
    stdout| +                              stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)

    stdout| +                              stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)

    stdout| +#!/usr/bin/env python3
    stdout| +"""run.py — selftest for tools/cur_pipeline.py (bundle -> evidence -> verdict, one command).
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Asserts the one-command entrypoint maps each fixture family to the expected grader exit and
    stdout| +transcript content, and that the extractor-reject path returns 2 without grading. Unique scratch
    stdout| +dirs keep concurrent invocations isolated. Exit 0 if all pass; non-zero otherwise.
    stdout| +"""
    stdout| +
    stdout| +import json
    stdout| +import os
    stdout| +import subprocess
    stdout| +import sys
    stdout| +import tempfile
    stdout| +
    stdout| +HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    stdout| +REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE)))
    stdout| +PIPELINE = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_pipeline.py")
    stdout| +FIXDIR = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "tests", "cur_extractor", "fixtures")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +# fixture -> expected pipeline exit / final.
    stdout| +EXPECT = {
    stdout| +    "e3-pass": 0,
    stdout| +    "e4-pass": 0,
    stdout| +    "e7-pass": 0,
    stdout| +    "e3-role-only-negative-control": 3,
    stdout| +    "e7-no-samples": 3,
    stdout| +    "e4-degree4-move": 1,
    stdout| +    "e7-preservation-fails": 1,
    stdout| +    "e3-not-in-kernel": 2,
    stdout| +    "malformed-duplicate-id": 2,
    stdout| +    "unsupported-contract": 2,
    stdout| +}
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def run_pipeline(bundle, scratch):
    stdout| +    mpath = os.path.join(scratch, "m.json")
    stdout| +    tpath = os.path.join(scratch, "t.txt")
    stdout| +    p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, PIPELINE, bundle, "--out", mpath, "--transcript", tpath],
    stdout| +                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    stdout| +    transcript = ""
    stdout| +    if os.path.exists(tpath):
    stdout| +        with open(tpath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +            transcript = f.read()
    stdout| +    return p.returncode, transcript, mpath
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def main():
    stdout| +    scratch = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="cur_pipeline_selftest_")
    stdout| +    failures = []
    stdout| +    total = 0
    stdout| +    ok = 0
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +    print("PIPELINE SELFTEST — tools/tests/cur_pipeline/run.py")
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    for key, expected_exit in EXPECT.items():
    stdout| +        total += 1
    stdout| +        bpath = os.path.join(FIXDIR, key + ".json")
    stdout| +        rc, transcript, _ = run_pipeline(bpath, scratch)
    stdout| +        passed = (rc == expected_exit)
    stdout| +        # Reject fixtures (extractor exit 2, no manifest) are NOT graded: transcript must NOT
    stdout| +        # contain a grader line and MUST note "not grading". Ambiguous/contradictory exits (exit 2
    stdout| +        # after grading) DO contain a grader line. Non-reject fixtures must contain a grader line.
    stdout| +        has_grader = "[grader]" in transcript
    stdout| +        has_pipeline_exit = "PIPELINE_EXIT=" in transcript
    stdout| +        is_reject = key in ("malformed-duplicate-id", "unsupported-contract")
    stdout| +        if is_reject:
    stdout| +            passed = passed and (not has_grader) and ("not grading" in transcript)
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            passed = passed and has_grader and has_pipeline_exit
    stdout| +        if passed:
    stdout| +            ok += 1
    stdout| +            status = "OK"
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            failures.append((key, "exit=%s expected=%s grader=%s pexit=%s" % (rc, expected_exit, has_grader, has_pipeline_exit)))
    stdout| +            status = "FAIL"
    stdout| +        print("  %-32s exit=%s expected=%s (%s)" % (key, rc, expected_exit, status))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- reject path: extractor exit 2 -> pipeline exit 2 and transcript notes "not grading".
    stdout| +    total += 1
    stdout| +    bpath = os.path.join(FIXDIR, "malformed-duplicate-id.json")
    stdout| +    rc, transcript, _ = run_pipeline(bpath, scratch)
    stdout| +    reject_ok = (rc == 2) and ("not grading" in transcript)
    stdout| +    if reject_ok:
    stdout| +        ok += 1
    stdout| +    else:
    stdout| +        failures.append(("reject-path", "exit=%s not_grading=%s" % (rc, "not grading" in transcript)))
    stdout| +    print("  %-32s exit=%s expect 2, not-grading note present (%s)" % ("reject-path", rc, "OK" if reject_ok else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    # ---- foreign working directory.
    stdout| +    total += 1
    stdout| +    extcwd = os.path.join(scratch, "outside")
    stdout| +    os.makedirs(extcwd, exist_ok=True)
    stdout| +    bpath = os.path.join(FIXDIR, "e3-pass.json")
    stdout| +    mpath = os.path.join(extcwd, "m.json")
    stdout| +    tpath = os.path.join(extcwd, "t.txt")
    stdout| +    p = subprocess.run([sys.executable, PIPELINE, bpath, "--out", mpath, "--transcript", tpath],
    stdout| +                       cwd=extcwd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    stdout| +    foreign_ok = (p.returncode == 0) and os.path.exists(mpath)
    stdout| +    if foreign_ok:
    stdout| +        ok += 1
    stdout| +    else:
    stdout| +        failures.append(("foreign-cwd", "exit=%s manifest=%s" % (p.returncode, os.path.exists(mpath))))
    stdout| +    print("  %-32s exit=%s (foreign cwd) (%s)" % ("foreign-cwd", p.returncode, "OK" if foreign_ok else "FAIL"))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    import shutil
    stdout| +    shutil.rmtree(scratch, ignore_errors=True)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +    print("SUMMARY: %d/%d pass, %d fail" % (ok, total, len(failures)))
    stdout| +    if failures:
    stdout| +        for label, detail in failures:
    stdout| +            print("    - %s: %s" % (label, detail))
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +    return 0 if len(failures) == 0 else 1
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +if __name__ == "__main__":
    stdout| +    sys.exit(main())

    stdout| new file mode 100644

    stdout| +++ b/tools/tests/cur_schema/run.py
    stdout| @@ -0,0 +1,293 @@
    stdout| +#!/usr/bin/env python3
    stdout| +"""run.py — selftests for tools/cur_artifact_schema.py.
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Exercises the schema checker, the two emitters, and the identity drift test:
    stdout| +
    stdout| +  * every current valid extractor fixture is schema-valid (exit 0), and matches the
    stdout| +    extractor's own accept/reject decision on the malformed set
    stdout| +  * schema rejection cases: duplicate ids, unknown contract, malformed collections,
    stdout| +    non-array citations, invalid numeric domains, E4 d/s-vs-e_in/e_out contradiction,
    stdout| +    E7 sequence-shorter-than-window, missing required structure
    stdout| +  * missing optional evidence remains schema-valid
    stdout| +  * the same schema-valid fixture still produces CANNOT_DETERMINE downstream (so schema
    stdout| +    validity is not mistaken for a verdict)
    stdout| +  * emit-json and emit-markdown are deterministic (byte-identical across runs)
    stdout| +  * foreign working directory
    stdout| +  * two concurrent schema checks
    stdout| +  * drift test: the schema module's pinned constants/identities match the extractor and
    stdout| +    grader they are bound to
    stdout| +
    stdout| +Exit 0 if all pass; nonzero otherwise. Never edits the extractor or grader.
    stdout| +"""
    stdout| +
    stdout| +import json
    stdout| +import os
    stdout| +import shutil
    stdout| +import subprocess
    stdout| +import sys
    stdout| +import tempfile
    stdout| +
    stdout| +HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    stdout| +REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE)))
    stdout| +SCHEMA_CMD = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_artifact_schema.py")
    stdout| +EXTRACTOR = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_extract_evidence.py")
    stdout| +GRADER = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_grade_artifact.py")
    stdout| +EXTRACTOR_FIXDIR = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "tests", "cur_extractor", "fixtures")
    stdout| +SCHEMA_FIXDIR = os.path.join(HERE, "fixtures")
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _run_argv(argv, cwd=None, env=None, timeout=60):
    stdout| +    merged = dict(os.environ)
    stdout| +    if env:
    stdout| +        merged.update(env)
    stdout| +    return subprocess.run([sys.executable] + argv, capture_output=True, text=True,
    stdout| +                          cwd=cwd, env=merged, timeout=timeout)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _schema_check(bundle_path, cwd=None):
    stdout| +    p = _run_argv([SCHEMA_CMD, "check", bundle_path], cwd=cwd)
    stdout| +    report = {}
    stdout| +    if p.stdout:
    stdout| +        try:
    stdout| +            report = json.loads(p.stdout)
    stdout| +        except ValueError:
    stdout| +            pass
    stdout| +    return p.returncode, report
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _schema_valid(bundle_path):
    stdout| +    rc, report = _schema_check(bundle_path)
    stdout| +    return rc == 0 and bool(report.get("schema_valid"))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _write(path, text):
    stdout| +    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +        f.write(text)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def main():
    stdout| +    scratch = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="cur_schema_selftest_")
    stdout| +    failures = []
    stdout| +    total = 0
    stdout| +    ok = 0
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    def check(label, cond, detail=""):
    stdout| +        nonlocal total, ok
    stdout| +        total += 1
    stdout| +        if cond:
    stdout| +            ok += 1
    stdout| +            print("  %-52s OK" % (label,))
    stdout| +        else:
    stdout| +            failures.append((label, detail))
    stdout| +            print("  %-52s FAIL  %s" % (label, detail))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +        print("SCHEMA SELFTEST — tools/tests/cur_schema/run.py")
    stdout| +        print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 1. every current valid extractor fixture is schema-valid.
    stdout| +        # The malformed trio (duplicate-id / nonarray-cites / unsupported-contract) is the
    stdout| +        # extractor-reject set and is asserted separately in test #2; exclude it here.
    stdout| +        malformed_set = ("malformed-duplicate-id.json", "malformed-nonarray-cites.json",
    stdout| +                         "unsupported-contract.json")
    stdout| +        extractor_fixtures = sorted(
    stdout| +            f for f in os.listdir(EXTRACTOR_FIXDIR)
    stdout| +            if f.endswith(".json") and f not in malformed_set)
    stdout| +        valid_ct = 0
    stdout| +        mismatch = []
    stdout| +        for fn in extractor_fixtures:
    stdout| +            path = os.path.join(EXTRACTOR_FIXDIR, fn)
    stdout| +            rc, report = _schema_check(path)
    stdout| +            if rc == 0 and report.get("schema_valid"):
    stdout| +                valid_ct += 1
    stdout| +            else:
    stdout| +                mismatch.append(fn)
    stdout| +        check("all current valid fixture families schema-valid (%d accepted)" % valid_ct,
    stdout| +              valid_ct == len(extractor_fixtures) and len(mismatch) == 0,
    stdout| +              "accepted=%s total=%s mismatch=%s" % (valid_ct, len(extractor_fixtures), mismatch))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 2. the malformed set is rejected by BOTH schema checker and extractor.
    stdout| +        malformed_agree = True
    stdout| +        for fn in ("malformed-duplicate-id.json", "malformed-nonarray-cites.json",
    stdout| +                   "unsupported-contract.json"):
    stdout| +            path = os.path.join(EXTRACTOR_FIXDIR, fn)
    stdout| +            sc_rc, _ = _schema_check(path)
    stdout| +            ext = _run_argv([EXTRACTOR, path, "--out", os.path.join(scratch, "x.json")])
    stdout| +            if not (sc_rc == 2 and ext.returncode == 2):
    stdout| +                malformed_agree = False
    stdout| +        check("malformed set rejected by schema checker AND extractor", malformed_agree)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 3. schema rejection fixtures.
    stdout| +        reject_cases = [
    stdout| +            "duplicate-node-id.json",
    stdout| +            "unknown-contract.json",
    stdout| +            "malformed-collections.json",
    stdout| +            "nonarray-cites.json",
    stdout| +            "invalid-numeric-domain.json",
    stdout| +            "e4-dual-form-contradiction.json",
    stdout| +            "e7-width-short-sequence.json",
    stdout| +            "missing-required-structure.json",
    stdout| +        ]
    stdout| +        rej_all = True
    stdout| +        for fn in reject_cases:
    stdout| +            rc, report = _schema_check(os.path.join(SCHEMA_FIXDIR, fn))
    stdout| +            if not (rc == 2 and not report.get("schema_valid")):
    stdout| +                rej_all = False
    stdout| +        check("rejection fixtures each exit 2 (schema-invalid)", rej_all)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 4. missing optional evidence stays schema-valid.
    stdout| +        opt_ok = _schema_valid(os.path.join(SCHEMA_FIXDIR, "missing-optional-evidence.json"))
    stdout| +        check("missing optional evidence remains schema-valid", opt_ok)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 5. canonical alternate forms: E4 d/s that agrees is accepted.
    stdout| +        dual_ok = _schema_valid(os.path.join(SCHEMA_FIXDIR, "e4-valid-dual-form.json"))
    stdout| +        check("E4 d/s alternate form (consistent) is schema-valid", dual_ok)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 6. schema-valid payload still produces CANNOT_DETERMINE downstream.
    stdout| +        # e7-params-only is schema-valid, but the grader derives no evidence bits.
    stdout| +        cd_path = os.path.join(SCHEMA_FIXDIR, "e7-params-only.json")
    stdout| +        cd_schema_rc, cd_report = _schema_check(cd_path)
    stdout| +        man_path = os.path.join(scratch, "cd_manifest.json")
    stdout| +        ext = _run_argv([EXTRACTOR, cd_path, "--out", man_path])
    stdout| +        grad = _run_argv([GRADER, man_path])
    stdout| +        grad_out = {}
    stdout| +        if grad.stdout:
    stdout| +            try:
    stdout| +                grad_out = json.loads(grad.stdout)
    stdout| +            except ValueError:
    stdout| +                pass
    stdout| +        cd_downstream = (cd_schema_rc == 0 and grad.returncode == 3
    stdout| +                         and grad_out.get("final") == "CANNOT_DETERMINE")
    stdout| +        check("schema-valid payload still yields CANNOT_DETERMINE downstream", cd_downstream,
    stdout| +              "schema_rc=%s grader_rc=%s final=%s" % (cd_schema_rc, grad.returncode, grad_out.get("final")))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 7. determinism of emit-json.
    stdout| +        a = _run_argv([SCHEMA_CMD, "emit-json"])
    stdout| +        b = _run_argv([SCHEMA_CMD, "emit-json"])
    stdout| +        check("emit-json deterministic", a.returncode == 0 and b.returncode == 0 and a.stdout == b.stdout)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 8. determinism of emit-markdown.
    stdout| +        md1 = _run_argv([SCHEMA_CMD, "emit-markdown"])
    stdout| +        md2 = _run_argv([SCHEMA_CMD, "emit-markdown"])
    stdout| +        check("emit-markdown deterministic", md1.returncode == 0 and md2.returncode == 0
    stdout| +              and md1.stdout == md2.stdout)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 9. emitted markdown carries no timestamp/date literal.
    stdout| +        has_date = False
    stdout| +        for line in md1.stdout.splitlines():
    stdout| +            for tok in ("20", "19"):
    stdout| +                if len(line) >= 10 and line.strip().startswith(tok):
    stdout| +                    has_date = True
    stdout| +        # A date literal looks like YYYY-MM-DD; scan for the pattern.
    stdout| +        import re
    stdout| +        has_date = bool(re.search(r"\b(?:20|19)\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{2}\b", md1.stdout))
    stdout| +        check("emit-markdown contains no timestamp/date literal", not has_date)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 10. foreign working directory.
    stdout| +        extcwd = os.path.join(scratch, "outside")
    stdout| +        os.makedirs(extcwd, exist_ok=True)
    stdout| +        fwd_rc, fd_report = _schema_check(os.path.join(SCHEMA_FIXDIR, "e7-params-only.json"), cwd=extcwd)
    stdout| +        check("foreign working directory", fwd_rc == 0 and bool(fd_report.get("schema_valid")))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 11. two concurrent schema checks.
    stdout| +        # Both children are LAUNCHED before either is awaited. The previous version called
    stdout| +        # the blocking helper twice in sequence, so the "concurrent" label described two
    stdout| +        # sequential runs. Overlap is now observed, not assumed: both handles are checked
    stdout| +        # for liveness after both have been started.
    stdout| +        cmd1 = [sys.executable, SCHEMA_CMD, "check",
    stdout| +                os.path.join(SCHEMA_FIXDIR, "e7-params-only.json")]
    stdout| +        cmd2 = [sys.executable, SCHEMA_CMD, "check",
    stdout| +                os.path.join(SCHEMA_FIXDIR, "missing-optional-evidence.json")]
    stdout| +        proc1 = subprocess.Popen(cmd1, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
    stdout| +        proc2 = subprocess.Popen(cmd2, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
    stdout| +        overlapped = (proc1.poll() is None) and (proc2.poll() is None)
    stdout| +        out1, _ = proc1.communicate(timeout=180)
    stdout| +        out2, _ = proc2.communicate(timeout=180)
    stdout| +        concurrent_ok = False
    stdout| +        try:
    stdout| +            r1 = json.loads(out1)
    stdout| +            r2 = json.loads(out2)
    stdout| +            concurrent_ok = (overlapped and proc1.returncode == 0 and proc2.returncode == 0
    stdout| +                             and bool(r1.get("schema_valid")) and bool(r2.get("schema_valid")))
    stdout| +        except ValueError:
    stdout| +            concurrent_ok = False
    stdout| +        check("two concurrent schema checks", concurrent_ok,
    stdout| +              "overlapped=%s rc=%s,%s" % (overlapped, proc1.returncode, proc2.returncode))
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 12. drift test: schema-module pinned constants/identities match extractor+grader.
    stdout| +        drift_ok = _run_drift_test()
    stdout| +        test_path = os.path.join(REPO, "tools", "cur_artifact_schema.py")
    stdout| +        drift_ok = drift_ok and _drift_by_import(test_path)
    stdout| +        check("drift test: schema pins match extractor/grader", drift_ok)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +        # ---- 13. unknown command rejected.
    stdout| +        unk = _run_argv([SCHEMA_CMD, "bogus"])
    stdout| +        check("unknown schema command rejected (exit 2)", unk.returncode == 2)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    finally:
    stdout| +        shutil.rmtree(scratch, ignore_errors=True)
    stdout| +
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +    print("SUMMARY: %d/%d pass, %d fail" % (ok, total, len(failures)))
    stdout| +    for label, detail in failures:
    stdout| +        print("    - %s: %s" % (label, detail))
    stdout| +    print("=" * 78)
    stdout| +    return 0 if len(failures) == 0 else 1
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _run_drift_test():
    stdout| +    """Structural drift: re-import the schema module and compare to extractor/grader in-process."""
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(REPO, "tools"))
    stdout| +        import importlib
    stdout| +        schema = importlib.import_module("cur_artifact_schema")
    stdout| +        extractor = importlib.import_module("cur_extract_evidence")
    stdout| +        grader = importlib.import_module("cur_grade_artifact")
    stdout| +        checks = [
    stdout| +            schema.SCHEMA_VERSION == grader.SCHEMA_VERSION,
    stdout| +            schema.RULESET_ID == grader.RULESET_ID,
    stdout| +            schema.BUNDLE_SCHEMA == extractor.BUNDLE_SCHEMA,
    stdout| +            schema.E3_N == extractor.E3_N,
    stdout| +            schema.E3_ODD_N == extractor.E3_ODD_N,
    stdout| +            schema.E4_TWO_HOUSES == extractor.E4_TWO_HOUSES,
    stdout| +            schema.E4_MAX_DEGREE == extractor.E4_MAX_DEGREE,
    stdout| +            schema.E7_WINDOW == extractor.E7_WINDOW,
    stdout| +            schema.E7_MODULUS == extractor.E7_MODULUS,
    stdout| +            schema.E7_ODD_WINDOW == extractor.E7_ODD_WINDOW,
    stdout| +            schema.PINNED_RUBRIC_REF == grader.PINNED_RUBRIC_REF,
    stdout| +            schema.PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS == grader.PINNED_CONTRACT_REFS,
    stdout| +            schema.PINNED_CONTRACT_COMMITS == extractor.PINNED_CONTRACT_COMMITS,
    stdout| +            schema.PINNED_CONTRACT_DIGESTS == extractor.PINNED_CONTRACT_DIGESTS,
    stdout| +            schema.PINNED_CONTRACT_IN_TREE == extractor.PINNED_CONTRACT_IN_TREE,
    stdout| +        ]
    stdout| +        return all(checks)
    stdout| +    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 host-tool test; re-raised by caller logic only
    stdout| +        print("    drift test import error: %s" % e)
    stdout| +        return False
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +def _drift_by_import(module_path):
    stdout| +    """Verify the module's own exports stay consistent with what emit-json surfaces."""
    stdout| +    try:
    stdout| +        with open(module_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    stdout| +            src = f.read()
    stdout| +        # The drift test already uses in-process imports; here assert the source is not
    stdout| +        # broken by emitting json and rebasing fields it references.
    stdout| +        js = _run_argv([SCHEMA_CMD, "emit-json"])
    stdout| +        if js.returncode != 0:
    stdout| +            return False
    stdout| +        data = json.loads(js.stdout)
    stdout| +        # regenerate via the real module to confirm the JSON matches its FIELD_TABLE length.
    stdout| +        sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(REPO, "tools"))
    stdout| +        import importlib
    stdout| +        schema = importlib.import_module("cur_artifact_schema")
    stdout| +        return len(data.get("fields", [])) == len(schema.FIELD_TABLE) and \
    stdout| +               data["bundle_schema"] == schema.BUNDLE_SCHEMA
    stdout| +    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
    stdout| +        print("    schema source drift error: %s" % e)
    stdout| +        return False
    stdout| +
    stdout| +
    stdout| +if __name__ == "__main__":
    stdout| +    sys.exit(main())
